# Counting Stock
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1001 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1001.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1001.desc
	theme = merchant
	override_background = bp1_wine_cellar

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = holding_tally_stick
		camera = camera_event_right_away
	}

	widgets = {
		widget = { gui = event_window_widget_vfx_background_flickering_candlelight_dark container = foreground_shader_vfx_container }
		widget = { gui = event_window_widget_vfx_left_character_flickering_candlelight_dark container = foreground_shader_vfx_container }
		widget = {
			gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		add_character_flag = {
			flag = use_sickness_clothes
			days = 7
		}
		gdp_tgf_setup_good_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1001.a
		add_stress = medium_stress_loss
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1001.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_merchant }
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = lifestyle_merchant
				track = creditor
				value = lifestyle_merchant_xp_gain_minor_value
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		remove_character_flag = use_sickness_clothes
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = market someone else owns, where $ROUTE$ sells a needed good. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_praise_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_praise_market_temp
	gdp_tgf_stranger_holder_market_trigger = yes
	any_trade_good_with_need = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_praise_good_temp
		$ROUTE$ = {
			is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
				trade_market = scope:tgf_praise_market_temp
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_praise_good_temp
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_praise_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_praise_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_praise_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_praise_route_temp }
	}
}

# Word Travels
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1002 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1002.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1002.desc
	theme = merchant

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = trade_deal
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_market_holder
		animation = royal_wave
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			highest_traded_good_category_xp_level >= gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_praise_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_praise_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_praise_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			random_trade_good_with_need = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_praise_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_praise_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1002.a
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:tgf_market_holder
			gold = minor_gold_value
		}
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1002.b
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -20
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1002.c
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# From the Source
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1003 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1003.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1003.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_base_metals
				scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_precious_metals
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_jewelry
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_smithy_workshop
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_wood
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_hemp
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_lacquerware
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_carpentry_workshop
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_leather
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_furs
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_dyes
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_tannery
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_glass
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_ceramics
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_textiles
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_artisan_street
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_oil }
		reference = bp1_wine_cellar
	}
	override_background = { reference = sitting_room }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = pondering
		camera = camera_event_left
	}

	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = writing
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_report_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		gdp_tgf_setup_buy_report_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1003.a
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1003.b
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1003.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = { culture = scope:tgf_source_culture }
		}
		scope:tgf_source_culture = {
			change_cultural_acceptance = {
				target = root.culture
				value = minor_cultural_acceptance_gain
				desc = cultural_acceptance_gain_event
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# An Open Road
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1004 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1004.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1004.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = merchant_gesturing
		camera = camera_event_right_away
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			is_commission_route = no
			has_trader = yes
			any_traded_good = { always = yes }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				is_commission_route = no
				has_trader = yes
				any_traded_good = { always = yes }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_traded_good = { save_scope_as = tgf_good }
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1004.a
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = content factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1004.b
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1004.c
		scope:tgf_route = {
			gdp_tgf_add_tiered_route_modifier_effect = {
				MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_heavier_loads
				YEARS = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = craven factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# Dreams of Profit
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1005 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1005.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1005.desc.livestock
			}
			desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1005.desc
		}
		desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1005.desc.outro
	}
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = bedchamber }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = laugh
		camera = camera_event_right_forward
	}

	widgets = {
		widget = { gui = event_window_widget_vfx_background_night_scene container = foreground_shader_vfx_container }
		widget = { gui = event_window_widget_vfx_left_character_night_scene container = foreground_shader_vfx_container }
		widget = {
			gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		add_character_flag = {
			flag = use_sickness_clothes
			days = 7
		}
		gdp_tgf_setup_good_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1005.a
		trigger = {
			can_create_task_contract = { type_name = promote_goods }
		}
		custom_tooltip = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1005.a.tt
		trigger_event = { on_action = gdp_promote_goods }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1005.b
		add_stress = minor_stress_gain
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = content factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1005.c
		add_stress = minor_stress_loss
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = diligent factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		remove_character_flag = use_sickness_clothes
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# Fools Who Check
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1006 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1006.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1006.desc
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = bp1_wine_cellar }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = trade_deal
		camera = camera_event_left_crowning_observation
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = arguing
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_report_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		gdp_tgf_setup_buy_report_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1006.a
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1006.b
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		stress_impact = {
			greedy = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade route with a trader that sells a good in a market with an origin county
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_crate_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_crate_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_route_sells_here_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_crate_route_temp }
	}
}

# Ask for It by Name
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1007 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1007.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1007.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = merchant_gesturing
		camera = camera_event_center
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:tgf_good }
			scripted_animation = gdp_tgf_good_animation
		}
		animation = merchant_box
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			highest_traded_good_category_xp_level >= gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		exists = house
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_crate_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_crate_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_route_sells_here_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_crate_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_crate_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = {
						save_scope_as = tgf_source_county
						culture = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_culture }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_trader
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1007.a
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_route = {
			gdp_tgf_add_tiered_route_modifier_effect = {
				MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_known_mark
				YEARS = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1007.b
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = market with a stranger holder, where $ROUTE$ buys or sells. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_judge_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_stranger_holder_market_trigger = yes
	OR = {
		gdp_tgf_route_buys_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
		gdp_tgf_route_sells_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	}
}

# this = route where a market has a foreign holder and trades one of the route's goods. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_judge_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_judge_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_judge_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_judge_route_temp }
	}
}

# The Appraiser
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1008 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1008.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1008.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = study }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_market_holder
		animation = worry
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			highest_traded_good_category_xp_level >= gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_judge_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_judge_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_judge_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_judge_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						OR = {
							is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
								trade_market = scope:tgf_market
								trade_good_type = scope:tgf_judge_good_temp
							}
							is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
								trade_market = scope:tgf_market
								trade_good_type = scope:tgf_judge_good_temp
							}
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1008.a
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 35
			}
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = deceitful factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1008.b
		trigger = {
			is_ai = no
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			pay_short_term_gold = {
				target = root
				gold = minor_gold_value
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = pleased_opinion
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1008.c
		trigger = {
			is_ai = no
			scope:tgf_market_holder = { short_term_gold >= medium_gold_value }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_miniscule_value
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			pay_short_term_gold = {
				target = root
				gold = medium_gold_value
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# Learn to Count
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1009 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1009.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1009.desc.livestock
			}
			desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1009.desc
		}
		desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1009.desc.outro
	}
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = bp1_wine_cellar }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = holding_tally_stick
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_heir
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:tgf_good }
			scripted_animation = gdp_tgf_good_animation
		}
		animation = merchant_box
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		exists = player_heir
		player_heir = {
			is_ai = yes
			age >= 8
			is_physically_able = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		gdp_tgf_setup_good_effect = yes
		player_heir = { save_scope_as = tgf_heir }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1009.a
		scope:tgf_heir = {
			add_stewardship_skill = 1
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_miniscule_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1009.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		scope:tgf_heir = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -10
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_merchant }
				add_trait_xp = {
					trait = lifestyle_merchant
					track = artisan
					value = lifestyle_merchant_xp_gain_minor_value
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = compassionate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ sells a good and an AI rival with an AI-headed house trades too
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_imitator_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_route_sells_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	any_potential_trade_rival_in_market = {
		target = root
		gdp_tgf_valid_rival_trigger = yes
		exists = house
		house.house_head ?= { is_ai = yes }
		NOT = { house = root.house }
	}
}

# this = trade route where a market has an AI rival from an AI-led house. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_imitator_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_imitator_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_imitator_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_imitator_route_temp }
	}
}

# The Imitator
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1010 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1010.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1010.desc.livestock
			}
			desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1010.desc
		}
		desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1010.desc.outro
	}
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = anger
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = arguing
		camera = camera_event_right
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_rival
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			highest_traded_good_category_xp_level >= gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		house.house_head ?= {
			OR = {
				this = root
				is_ai = yes
			}
		}
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_imitator_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_imitator_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_imitator_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_imitator_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_imitator_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			random_potential_trade_rival_in_market = {
				target = root
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_valid_rival_trigger = yes
					exists = house
					house.house_head ?= { is_ai = yes }
					NOT = { house = root.house }
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_rival
			}
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_rival = {
			random_held_title = {
				limit = { gdp_merchant_company_title_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = tgf_rival_company
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_trader
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1010.a
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_rival = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -20
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = forgiving factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1010.b
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_rival = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -20
			}
		}
		house = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_house_relation_with = scope:tgf_rival.house }
				}
				set_house_relation = {
					target = scope:tgf_rival.house
					level = quarrel
					description = house_relation_tgf_imitation_desc
				}
			}
			else = {
				change_house_relation_effect = {
					HOUSE = scope:tgf_rival.house
					VALUE = house_relation_damage_minor_value
					REASON = tgf_imitation
					CHAR = root
					TARGET_CHAR = scope:tgf_rival
					TITLE = scope:tgf_rival_company
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1010.c
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = vengeful factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = a rival with money on hand and an adult to send, since we create no one for the errand
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_supplier_rival_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_valid_rival_trigger = yes
	short_term_gold >= medium_gold_value
	OR = {
		any_trade_route = {
			has_trader = yes
			trader ?= { is_physically_able_adult = yes }
		}
		any_courtier = { is_physically_able_adult = yes }
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ buys a good and a rival with means trades too. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_supplier_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_route_buys_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	any_potential_trade_rival_in_market = {
		target = root
		gdp_tgf_supplier_rival_trigger = yes
	}
}

# this = trade route with a supplier market on it. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_supplier_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_supplier_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_supplier_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_supplier_route_temp }
	}
}

# The Price of Names
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1011 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1011.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1011.desc
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = bp1_wine_cellar }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = biting_coin
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_envoy
		animation = bribing
		camera = camera_event_right
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_rival
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			highest_traded_good_category_xp_level >= gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_supplier_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_supplier_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_supplier_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_supplier_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_supplier_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			random_potential_trade_rival_in_market = {
				target = root
				limit = { gdp_tgf_supplier_rival_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = tgf_rival
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_rival = {
			random_held_title = {
				limit = { gdp_merchant_company_title_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = tgf_rival_company
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_trade_route = {
						has_trader = yes
						trader ?= { is_physically_able_adult = yes }
					}
				}
				random_trade_route = {
					limit = {
						has_trader = yes
						trader ?= { is_physically_able_adult = yes }
					}
					trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_envoy }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_courtier = {
					limit = { is_physically_able_adult = yes }
					save_scope_as = tgf_envoy
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1011.a
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
		scope:tgf_rival = {
			pay_short_term_gold = {
				target = root
				gold = medium_gold_value
			}
			gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
				CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
				VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_massive_value
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = paranoid factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1011.b
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = buy_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = -0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		gdp_tgf_add_tiered_character_modifier_effect = {
			MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_kept_edge
			YEARS = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = an ally merchant candidate who does not resent root. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_valid_ally_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_valid_rival_trigger = yes
	exists = house
	house.house_head ?= { is_ai = yes }
	NOT = { house = root.house }
	opinion = {
		target = root
		value >= 0
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ sells a good that a friendly AI merchant also sells
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_ally_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_ally_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_ally_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_ally_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_ally_good_temp
				}
			}
			root = {
				any_potential_trade_ally_on_routes = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_ally_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_ally_good_temp
					gdp_tgf_valid_ally_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_ally_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_ally_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_ally_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_ally_route_temp }
	}
}

# A Truce of Traders
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1012 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1012.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1012.desc
	theme = merchant

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = trade_deal
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_ally
		animation = merchant_gesturing
		camera = camera_event_very_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		house.house_head ?= {
			OR = {
				this = root
				is_ai = yes
			}
		}
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_ally_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_ally_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_ally_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_ally_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_ally_good_temp
						}
					}
					root = {
						any_potential_trade_ally_on_routes = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_ally_good_temp
							gdp_tgf_valid_ally_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		random_potential_trade_ally_on_routes = {
			trade_market = scope:tgf_market
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			limit = { gdp_tgf_valid_ally_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_ally
		}
		scope:tgf_ally = {
			random_held_title = {
				limit = { gdp_merchant_company_title_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = tgf_ally_company
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1012.a
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = scope:tgf_ally
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_ally = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
		house = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_house_relation_with = scope:tgf_ally.house }
				}
				set_house_relation = {
					target = scope:tgf_ally.house
					level = cordial
					description = house_relation_tgf_truce_desc
				}
			}
			else = {
				change_house_relation_effect = {
					HOUSE = scope:tgf_ally.house
					VALUE = house_relation_improve_minor_value
					REASON = tgf_truce
					CHAR = root
					TARGET_CHAR = scope:tgf_ally
					TITLE = scope:tgf_ally_company
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = ambitious factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1012.b
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = -0.02
				years = 10
			}
		}
		gdp_tgf_add_tiered_character_modifier_effect = {
			MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_price_war
			YEARS = 10
		}
		scope:tgf_ally = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -15
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = content factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = market with a stranger holder, where $ROUTE$ buys. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_private_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_stranger_holder_market_trigger = yes
	gdp_tgf_route_buys_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
}

# this = trade route where a foreign holder's market sells a good the route buys. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_private_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_private_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_private_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_private_route_temp }
	}
}

# A Private Price
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1013 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1013.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1013.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = study }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = trade_deal
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_market_holder
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			highest_traded_good_category_xp_level >= gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_private_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_private_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_private_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_private_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_private_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1013.a
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.05 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = buy_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = 0.025
			years = 10
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1013.b
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = fledgling buy market with a graying commoner in the local pool
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_oldhands_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_fledgling_buy_market_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_oldhands_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_oldhands_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
			age >= 40
			is_physically_able_adult = yes
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route where an old porter waits at a fledgling buy market
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_oldhands_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_oldhands_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_oldhands_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_oldhands_route_temp }
	}
}

# Ask the Old Hands

		camera = camera_event_right
	}

		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"

			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_oldhands_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }

							trade_market = scope:tgf_market

						}

					trade_good_need_category = {
						save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_fledgling_cat_temp
					}
					root = {
						gdp_tgf_fledgling_in_category_trigger = { CATEGORY = scope:tgf_fledgling_cat_temp }
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}

		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_healthy = yes
				has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
				age >= 40
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_porter
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1014.a
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:tgf_porter
			gold = miniscule_gold_value
		}
		scope:tgf_porter = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
		add_courtier = scope:tgf_porter
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_major_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1014.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# A Beginner's Mistake
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1015 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1015.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1015.desc
	theme = merchant

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = mental_anguish
		camera = camera_event_table_left
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			lowest_traded_good_category_xp_level < gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		short_term_gold >= minor_gold_value
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_fledgling_buy_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_fledgling_buy_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_fledgling_buy_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_fledgling_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_fledgling_good_temp
						}
					}
					trade_good_need_category = {
						save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_fledgling_cat_temp
					}
					root = {
						gdp_tgf_fledgling_in_category_trigger = { CATEGORY = scope:tgf_fledgling_cat_temp }
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1015.a
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_major_value
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_merchant }
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = lifestyle_merchant
				track = artisan
				value = lifestyle_merchant_xp_gain_minor_value
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1015.b
		add_stress = minor_stress_loss
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = diligent factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = character who owns the handbook artifact of $HANDBOOK$ (practice, roads or mirror)
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_owns_handbook_trigger = {
	any_character_artifact = { var:tgf_handbook ?= flag:$HANDBOOK$ }
}

# Creates the masterwork handbook named by scope:tgf_handbook, tagged so the same title is never found twice
scripted_effect gdp_tgf_create_handbook_effect = {
	gdp_tgf_roll_artifact_rarity_effect = { RARITY = masterwork }
	if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_handbook = flag:practice }
		create_artifact = {
			name = gdp_tgf_handbook_practice_artifact
			description = gdp_tgf_handbook_practice_artifact_desc
			type = journal
			visuals = book
			modifier = artifact_trade_category_all_xp_gain_mult_1_modifier
			modifier = artifact_stewardship_1_modifier
			quality = scope:quality
			wealth = scope:wealth
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_handbook = flag:roads }
		create_artifact = {
			name = gdp_tgf_handbook_roads_artifact
			description = gdp_tgf_handbook_roads_artifact_desc
			type = journal
			visuals = book
			modifier = artifact_trade_category_all_xp_gain_mult_1_modifier
			modifier = artifact_stewardship_1_modifier
			quality = scope:quality
			wealth = scope:wealth
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_handbook = flag:mirror }
		create_artifact = {
			name = gdp_tgf_handbook_mirror_artifact
			description = gdp_tgf_handbook_mirror_artifact_desc
			type = journal
			visuals = book
			modifier = artifact_trade_category_all_xp_gain_mult_1_modifier
			modifier = artifact_stewardship_1_modifier
			quality = scope:quality
			wealth = scope:wealth
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		}
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact ?= {
		set_variable = {
			name = tgf_handbook
			value = scope:tgf_handbook
		}
	}
	gdp_tgf_add_artifact_trade_modifiers_effect = yes
}

# The Merchant's Handbook
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1016 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1016.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1016.desc.intro_livestock
			}
			desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1016.desc.intro
		}
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:tgf_handbook = flag:practice }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1016.desc.book_1
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:tgf_handbook = flag:roads }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1016.desc.book_2
			}
			desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1016.desc.book_3
		}
		desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1016.desc.outro
	}
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = study }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = reading
		camera = camera_event_left
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			lowest_traded_good_category_xp_level < gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		NOR = {
			gdp_tgf_owns_handbook_trigger = { HANDBOOK = practice }
			gdp_tgf_owns_handbook_trigger = { HANDBOOK = roads }
			gdp_tgf_owns_handbook_trigger = { HANDBOOK = mirror }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		gdp_tgf_setup_good_effect = yes
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { gdp_tgf_owns_handbook_trigger = { HANDBOOK = practice } } }
				save_scope_value_as = { name = tgf_handbook value = flag:practice }
			}
			1 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { gdp_tgf_owns_handbook_trigger = { HANDBOOK = roads } } }
				save_scope_value_as = { name = tgf_handbook value = flag:roads }
			}
			1 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { gdp_tgf_owns_handbook_trigger = { HANDBOOK = mirror } } }
				save_scope_value_as = { name = tgf_handbook value = flag:mirror }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1016.a
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_major_value
		}
		gdp_tgf_create_handbook_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1016.b
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = curious factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = fledgling buy market with a commoner seller in the local pool
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_sample_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_fledgling_buy_market_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_sample_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_sample_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
			is_physically_able_adult = yes
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route where a local seller waits at a fledgling buy market
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_sample_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_sample_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_sample_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_sample_route_temp }
	}
}

# The Feel of It
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1017 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1017.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1017.desc.livestock
			}
			desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1017.desc
		}
		desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1017.desc.outro
	}
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = pondering
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_seller
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:tgf_good }
			scripted_animation = gdp_tgf_good_animation
		}
		animation = merchant_box
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			lowest_traded_good_category_xp_level < gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_sample_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_sample_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_sample_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_fledgling_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_fledgling_good_temp
						}
					}
					trade_good_need_category = {
						save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_fledgling_cat_temp
					}
					root = {
						gdp_tgf_fledgling_in_category_trigger = { CATEGORY = scope:tgf_fledgling_cat_temp }
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_healthy = yes
				has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_seller
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_seller
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1017.a
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:tgf_seller
			gold = minor_gold_value
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_merchant }
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = lifestyle_merchant
				track = artisan
				value = lifestyle_merchant_xp_gain_minor_value
			}
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_massive_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1017.b
		add_prestige = miniscule_prestige_gain
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_miniscule_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = shy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = sell market whose county culture differs from root's
# We leave out livestock, since it is sold by the head and not by measure
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_measure_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_measure_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	trade_market_owner.title_province.county.culture = {
		NOT = { this = root.culture }
	}
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_measure_cat_temp }
			NOT = { scope:tgf_measure_cat_temp = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden }
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_measure_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_measure_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_measure_good_temp
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route where a sell market uses another culture's measures. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_measure_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_measure_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_measure_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_measure_route_temp }
	}
}

# Wrong Measure
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1018 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1018.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1018.desc
	theme = merchant

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = pondering
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			lowest_traded_good_category_xp_level < gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_measure_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_measure_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_measure_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					trade_good_need_category = { save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_measure_cat_temp }
					NOT = { scope:tgf_measure_cat_temp = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden }
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_measure_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_measure_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = {
						save_scope_as = tgf_source_county
						culture = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_culture }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1018.a
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_major_value
		}
		stress_impact = {
			greedy = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1018.b
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		add_gold = miniscule_gold_value
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_merchant }
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = lifestyle_merchant
				track = creditor
				value = lifestyle_merchant_xp_gain_minor_value
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1018.c
		add_prestige = miniscule_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_source_culture = {
			change_cultural_acceptance = {
				target = root.culture
				value = minor_cultural_acceptance_gain
				desc = cultural_acceptance_gain_event
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = shy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade good that $ROUTE$ sells in some market on it
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_bundle_sold_on_route_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_bundle_sold_good_temp
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_trade_market_on_route = {
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_bundle_sold_market_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_bundle_sold_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_bundle_sold_good_temp
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Either order of $X$ and $Y$. Expects scope:tgf_pair_a_temp and scope:tgf_pair_b_temp
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = {
	OR = {
		AND = {
			scope:tgf_pair_a_temp = trade_good_type:trade_good_$X$
			scope:tgf_pair_b_temp = trade_good_type:trade_good_$Y$
		}
		AND = {
			scope:tgf_pair_a_temp = trade_good_type:trade_good_$Y$
			scope:tgf_pair_b_temp = trade_good_type:trade_good_$X$
		}
	}
}

# this = trade good that makes a natural pair with $OTHER$, so the buyer wants both together
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_pair_a_temp
	$OTHER$ = { save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_pair_b_temp }
	OR = {
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = salt Y = fish }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = salt Y = meat }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = salt Y = cheese }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = alum Y = dyes }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = dyes Y = wool }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = dyes Y = cotton }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = dyes Y = silk }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = dyes Y = textiles }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = beer Y = grain }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = wine Y = cheese }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = wood Y = hemp }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = iron Y = wood }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = spices Y = meat }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = gems Y = gold }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = gems Y = silver }
		gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_match_trigger = { X = leather Y = books_paper }
	}
}

# this = good $ROUTE$ sells in $MARKET$, while root also trades another. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_bundle_first_good_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_bundle_good_temp
	$ROUTE$ = {
		is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
			trade_market = $MARKET$
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_bundle_good_temp
		}
	}
	root = {
		any_trade_route = {
			is_commission_route = no
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_bundle_other_route_temp
			any_traded_good = {
				NOT = { this = scope:tgf_bundle_good_temp }
				gdp_tgf_bundle_sold_on_route_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_bundle_other_route_temp }
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = market a stranger holds, where $ROUTE$ sells and root trades a second good on any route
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_bundle_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_bundle_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	gdp_tgf_stranger_holder_market_trigger = yes
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			gdp_tgf_bundle_first_good_trigger = {
				ROUTE = $ROUTE$
				MARKET = scope:tgf_bundle_market_temp
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_bundle_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_bundle_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_bundle_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_bundle_route_temp }
	}
}

# Two Goods, One Buyer
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1019 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1019.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { exists = scope:tgf_bundle_paired }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1019.desc
			}
			desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1019.desc.generic
		}
		desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1019.desc.outro
	}
	theme = trade

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = donkey_standing_baggage
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_market_holder
		animation = merchant_box
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_good_traded_in = { count >= 2 }
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_bundle_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_bundle_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_bundle_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_bundle_first_good_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						MARKET = scope:tgf_market
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		every_trade_route = {
			limit = { is_commission_route = no }
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_bundle_other_route_temp
			every_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { this = scope:tgf_good }
					gdp_tgf_bundle_sold_on_route_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_bundle_other_route_temp }
				}
				add_to_list = tgf_bundle_candidates
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_in_list = {
					list = tgf_bundle_candidates
					gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_trigger = { OTHER = scope:tgf_good }
				}
			}
			random_in_list = {
				list = tgf_bundle_candidates
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_bundle_pair_trigger = { OTHER = scope:tgf_good }
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good_b
			}
			save_scope_value_as = {
				name = tgf_bundle_paired
				value = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_in_list = {
				list = tgf_bundle_candidates
				save_scope_as = tgf_good_b
			}
		}
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				is_commission_route = no
				save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_bundle_b_route_temp
				scope:tgf_good_b = {
					gdp_tgf_bundle_sold_on_route_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_bundle_b_route_temp }
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route_b
		}
		scope:tgf_route_b = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_bundle_b_market_temp
					scope:tgf_route_b = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_bundle_b_market_temp
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good_b
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market_b
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1019.a
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market_b = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good_b
				value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1019.b
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = patient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade good used in construction
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_construction_good_trigger = {
	OR = {
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_stone
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_wood
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_hemp
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_iron
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_copper
	}
}

# this = trade market with a foreign holder building a holding, where $ROUTE$ sells a construction good
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_builders_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_builders_market_temp
	trade_market_owner ?= {
		exists = title_province.county
		title_province = {
			has_ongoing_holding_construction = yes
		}
	}
	gdp_tgf_stranger_holder_market_trigger = yes
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			gdp_tgf_construction_good_trigger = yes
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_builders_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_builders_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_builders_good_temp
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route where a building holder's market buys its construction goods
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_builders_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_builders_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_builders_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_builders_route_temp }
	}
}

scripted_effect gdp_tgf_builders_rate_apply_effect = {
	scope:tgf_build_county = {
		if = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_county_held_by_root_or_ai_trigger = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { scope:tgf_build_province = { has_holding_type = castle_holding } }
				add_county_modifier = {
					modifier = gdp_tgf_builders_rate_castle_$SIZE$_modifier
					years = $YEARS$
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { scope:tgf_build_province = { has_holding_type = church_holding } }
				add_county_modifier = {
					modifier = gdp_tgf_builders_rate_church_$SIZE$_modifier
					years = $YEARS$
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { scope:tgf_build_province = { has_holding_type = city_holding } }
				add_county_modifier = {
					modifier = gdp_tgf_builders_rate_city_$SIZE$_modifier
					years = $YEARS$
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_county_modifier = {
					modifier = gdp_tgf_builders_rate_generic_$SIZE$_modifier
					years = $YEARS$
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Applies the builder's-rate county modifier for the market holding type, doubled when root is renowned in scope:tgf_category
# $SIZE$ is full or small. Expects scope:tgf_build_county and scope:tgf_build_province
scripted_effect gdp_tgf_builders_rate_modifier_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { gdp_tgf_root_renowned_in_event_category_trigger = yes }
		gdp_tgf_builders_rate_apply_effect = {
			SIZE = $SIZE$_renowned
			YEARS = 10
		}
	}
	else = {
		gdp_tgf_builders_rate_apply_effect = {
			SIZE = $SIZE$
			YEARS = 5
		}
	}
}

gdp_trade_good_flavor.1020 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1020.t

		camera = camera_event_very_right
	}

		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"

		is_available_allow_travelling = yes

			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_builders_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_construction_good_trigger = yes

						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_builders_good_temp

					}

		}

				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }

					save_scope_as = tgf_build_province

			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:tgf_build_province = { has_holding_type = church_holding }
			}
			add_piety = miniscule_piety_gain
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_fledgling_in_category_trigger = { CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category }
			}
			gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
				CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
				VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1020.b
		gdp_tgf_builders_rate_modifier_effect = { SIZE = small }
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1020.c
		gdp_tgf_builders_rate_modifier_effect = { SIZE = full }
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }

			}
		}

		}

			MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_keep_the_best
			YEARS = 10
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {

	}

# this = character whose company knows $CATEGORY$ well enough to study its goods
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_studied_in_category_trigger = {
	any_held_title = {
		gdp_merchant_company_title_trigger = yes
		trade_good_category_level = {
			target = $CATEGORY$
			value >= gdp_tgf_study_expertise_level_value
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route that carries a good root has studied. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_study_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	any_traded_good = {
		trade_good_need_category = { save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_study_cat_temp }
		root = {
			gdp_tgf_studied_in_category_trigger = { CATEGORY = scope:tgf_study_cat_temp }
		}
	}
}

					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1021.a
		gdp_tgf_create_study_curio_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100

# this = trade route that carries a good whose category root is renowned in. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_nickname_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	any_trade_market_on_route = { exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county }
	any_traded_good = {
		trade_good_need_category = { save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_nickname_cat_temp }
		root = {
			gdp_tgf_renowned_in_category_trigger = { CATEGORY = scope:tgf_nickname_cat_temp }
		}
	}
}

# Grants the per-good trade nickname. Expects scope:tgf_good
scripted_effect gdp_tgf_trade_nickname_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_grain }
		set_nickname_effect = { NICKNAME = nick_tgf_grain }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_millet }
		set_nickname_effect = { NICKNAME = nick_tgf_millet }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_rice }
		set_nickname_effect = { NICKNAME = nick_tgf_rice }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_meat }
		set_nickname_effect = { NICKNAME = nick_tgf_meat }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_fish }
		set_nickname_effect = { NICKNAME = nick_tgf_fish }
	}
	else_if = {
