# A Name for the Trade
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1022 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1022.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1022.desc
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = biting_coin
		camera = camera_event_left
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			highest_traded_good_category_xp_level >= 75
		}
	}

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = gdp_tgf_had_event_1022 }
		OR = {
			has_any_nickname = no
			has_bad_nickname = yes
		}
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		highest_traded_good_category_xp_level >= 50
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_nickname_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		add_character_flag = gdp_tgf_had_event_1022
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_nickname_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					trade_good_need_category = { save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_nickname_cat_temp }
					root = {
						gdp_tgf_renowned_in_category_trigger = { CATEGORY = scope:tgf_nickname_cat_temp }
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = root
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1022.a
		gdp_tgf_trade_nickname_effect = yes
		add_prestige = major_prestige_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1022.b
		gdp_tgf_trade_nickname_effect = yes
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ buys a good from a stranger's county and the local pool holds a foreman
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_supply_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_stranger_holder_market_trigger = yes
	gdp_tgf_route_buys_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_supply_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_supply_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			is_physically_able_adult = yes
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_supply_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_supply_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_supply_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_supply_route_temp }
	}
}

# A Choke on Supply
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1023 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1023.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1023.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_base_metals
				scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_precious_metals
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_jewelry
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_smithy_workshop
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_wood
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_hemp
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_lacquerware
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_carpentry_workshop
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_leather
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_furs
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_dyes
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_tannery
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_glass
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_ceramics
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_textiles
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_artisan_street
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_oil }
		reference = bp1_wine_cellar
	}
	override_background = { reference = farmland }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = pondering
		camera = camera_event_left_away_3_4
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_foreman
		animation = merchant_gesturing
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_supply_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_supply_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_supply_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_supply_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_supply_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
				is_healthy = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_foreman
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_foreman
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1023.a
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		scope:tgf_source_county = {
			if = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_county_held_by_root_or_ai_trigger = yes }
				gdp_tgf_add_tiered_county_modifier_effect = {
					MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_extra_hands
					YEARS = 10
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10 # Dont stack too many goods modifiers
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1023.b
		add_courtier = scope:tgf_foreman
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 30
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1023.c
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ trades a good and an AI rival trades too
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_auction_market_trigger = {
	OR = {
		gdp_tgf_route_buys_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
		gdp_tgf_route_sells_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	}
	any_potential_trade_rival_in_market = {
		target = root
		gdp_tgf_auction_rival_trigger = yes
	}
}

# this = trade route
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_auction_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_auction_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_auction_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_auction_route_temp }
	}
}

# Under the Hammer
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1024 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1024.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1024.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			scope:tgf_source_province = { geographical_region = mediteranean_coast }
		}
		reference = gdp_italian_market
	}
	override_background = { reference = market_crowd }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = betting
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_rival
		animation = wedding_objection_start
		camera = camera_event_very_left_fov35
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_auction_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_auction_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_auction_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_auction_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						OR = {
							is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
								trade_market = scope:tgf_market
								trade_good_type = scope:tgf_auction_good_temp
							}
							is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
								trade_market = scope:tgf_market
								trade_good_type = scope:tgf_auction_good_temp
							}
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			random_potential_trade_rival_in_market = {
				target = root
				limit = { gdp_tgf_auction_rival_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = tgf_rival
			}
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_rival = {
			random_held_title = {
				limit = { gdp_merchant_company_title_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = tgf_rival_company
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_rival
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1024.a
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		gdp_tgf_add_tiered_character_modifier_effect = {
			MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_outbid
			YEARS = 10
		}
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		scope:tgf_rival = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -15
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = -0.01
				years = 10
			}
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = scope:tgf_rival
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = 0.01
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1024.b
		scope:tgf_rival = {
			remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -30
			}
		}
		add_intrigue_lifestyle_xp = minor_lifestyle_xp
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = scope:tgf_rival
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = -0.01
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1024.c
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_miniscule_value
		}
		gdp_tgf_add_tiered_character_modifier_effect = {
			MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_watched_bidding
			YEARS = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = coastal trade market where $ROUTE$ sells a good and the local pool holds someone to run the docks
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_quay_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_route_sells_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		is_coastal = yes
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_quay_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_quay_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			is_physically_able_adult = yes
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_quay_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_quay_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_quay_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_quay_route_temp }
	}
}

# First at the Quay
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1025 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1025.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1025.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = docks }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = bribing
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_docks_boss
		animation = personality_greedy
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_quay_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_quay_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_quay_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_quay_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_quay_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
				is_healthy = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_docks_boss
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_docks_boss
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1025.a
		trigger = { short_term_gold >= medium_gold_value }
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:tgf_docks_boss
			gold = medium_gold_value
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1025.b
		add_gold = medium_gold_value
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = -0.01
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = patient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1025.c
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}
	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ sells a non-livestock good and the local pool holds a stallholder
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_handful_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_handful_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			gdp_tgf_not_livestock_good_trigger = yes
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_handful_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_handful_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_handful_good_temp
				}
			}
		}
	}
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_handful_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_handful_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			is_physically_able_adult = yes
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_handful_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_handful_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_handful_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_handful_route_temp }
	}
}

# By the Handful
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1026 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1026.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1026.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = gdp_artisan_street_crowd }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_stallholder
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:tgf_good }
			scripted_animation = gdp_tgf_good_animation
		}
		animation = merchant_box
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_handful_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_handful_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_handful_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_not_livestock_good_trigger = yes
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_handful_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_handful_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
				is_healthy = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_stallholder
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_stallholder
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1026.a
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.03 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_source_county = {
			if = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_county_held_by_root_or_ai_trigger = yes }
				add_county_modifier = {
					modifier = gdp_tgf_bled_by_the_handful_modifier
					years = 10
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_stallholder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = compassionate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1026.b
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = -0.01
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_source_county = {
			if = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_county_held_by_root_or_ai_trigger = yes }
				change_prosperity_progress = minor_prosperity_progress_gain
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			greedy = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1026.c
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = patient factor = 0 }
		}
	}
	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

gdp_trade_good_flavor.1027 = {
	type = character_event

					}
				}

				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }

		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_trade_market_modifier = {
				modifier = gdp_tgf_market_day_crowds_modifier
				years = 10

				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1027.b

# this = market where $ROUTE$ sells and the pool holds a buyer of another heritage than root's
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_coin_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_route_sells_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_coin_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_coin_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			is_physically_able_adult = yes
			NOT = { culture ?= { has_same_culture_heritage = root.culture } }
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_coin_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_coin_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_coin_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_coin_route_temp }
	}
}

# Foreign Coin
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1028 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1028.t

	left_portrait = {

		camera = camera_event_left

		camera = camera_event_right_pointing_right
	}

		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"

					scope:tgf_route = {

							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_coin_good_temp
						}
					}

				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province

		}
		random_pool_character = {

			limit = {
				is_healthy = yes
				is_physically_able_adult = yes

			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_buyer
		}
		random_trade_good_traded_in = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { this = scope:tgf_good }
				exists = trade_good_need_category
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_good_b
		}

			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }

			CHAR = scope:tgf_buyer

			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = stubborn factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1028.b
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.005 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_buyer = {
			add_opinion = {

				opinion = -10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {

		trigger = { exists = scope:tgf_good_b }

			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category_b }

				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good_b
				value = { value = -0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}

			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}
	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade route with a trader that has run for three years
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_old_road_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	trader ?= {
		NOT = { this = root }
	}
	age_in_months >= 36
	any_traded_good = { always = yes }
}

# Years on the Road
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1029 = {

			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1029.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		scope:tgf_trader = {
			add_stewardship_skill = 2
			add_stress = massive_stress_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = temperate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1029.c
		scope:tgf_trader = {
			add_stewardship_skill = 2
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_further_roads_renowned_modifier } }
			if = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_root_renowned_in_event_category_trigger = yes }
				remove_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_further_roads_modifier
				add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_further_roads_renowned_modifier
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_further_roads_modifier } }
				add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_further_roads_modifier
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = content factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade good in a category root is renowned in, which $ROUTE$ sells in a market with a county
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_renowned_sold_good_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_renowned_good_temp
	trade_good_need_category = { save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_renowned_cat_temp }
	root = {
		gdp_tgf_renowned_in_category_trigger = { CATEGORY = scope:tgf_renowned_cat_temp }
	}
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_trade_market_on_route = {
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_renowned_market_temp
			exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
			trade_market_owner.title_province = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_watching_province_temp
				any_pool_character = {
					province = scope:tgf_watching_province_temp
					is_healthy = yes
					is_physically_able_adult = yes
				}
			}
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_renowned_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_renowned_good_temp
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route that sells a good root is renowned in
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_watching_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_watching_route_temp
	any_traded_good = {
		gdp_tgf_renowned_sold_good_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_watching_route_temp }
	}
}

# this = character who already has Ear to the Market in some category
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_has_ear_to_the_market_trigger = {
	OR = {
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_alcohol_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_comforts_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_cereals_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_meat_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_beasts_of_burden_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_salt_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_clothes_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_fabrics_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_dyes_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_alum_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_stone_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_carpentry_supplies_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_base_metals_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_luxury_items_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_jewelry_materials_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_precious_metals_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_medicaments_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_incense_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_luxury_consumption_modifier
		has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_books_modifier
	}
}

# Ear to the Market for scope:tgf_category, the category of the event's good
scripted_effect gdp_tgf_add_ear_to_the_market_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_alcohol }
		add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_alcohol_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_comforts }
		add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_comforts_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_cereals }
		add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_cereals_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_meat }
		add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_meat_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden }
		add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_ear_beasts_of_burden_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_salt }

	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_clothes }

	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_fabrics }

	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_dyes }

	}
	else_if = {

	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_stone }

	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_carpentry_supplies }

	}
	else_if = {

	else_if = {

	else_if = {

# Watching It Sell
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1030 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1030.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1030.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = trade_deal
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_buyer
		animation = pointing
		camera = camera_event_left_away_3_4
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			highest_traded_good_category_xp_level >= gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		exists = house
		gdp_tgf_has_ear_to_the_market_trigger = no
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_watching_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_watching_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_renowned_sold_good_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_watching_market_temp
					exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
					trade_market_owner.title_province = {
						save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_watching_province_temp
						any_pool_character = {
							province = scope:tgf_watching_province_temp
							is_healthy = yes
							is_physically_able_adult = yes
						}
					}
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_watching_market_temp
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
				is_healthy = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_buyer
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_buyer
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1030.a
		gdp_tgf_add_ear_to_the_market_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1030.b
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}
	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ sells a good and the local pool holds two adults to invest
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_investors_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_route_sells_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_investors_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_investors_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			is_physically_able_adult = yes
			count >= 2
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route with a trader that has run for at least two years
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_investors_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	age_in_months >= 24
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_investors_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_investors_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_investors_route_temp }
	}
}

# Outside Money
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1031 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1031.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1031.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_investor_a
		animation = holding_yoke
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_investor_b
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		exists = house
		any_trade_route = {
			count >= 2
			is_commission_route = no
		}
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_investors_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_investors_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_investors_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_outside_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_outside_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder ?= { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
				is_healthy = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_investor_a
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_healthy = yes
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
				NOT = { this = scope:tgf_investor_a }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_investor_b
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_investor_a
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_source_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1031.a
		add_gold = medium_gold_value
		scope:tgf_route = {
			gdp_tgf_add_tiered_route_modifier_effect = {
				MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_outside_capital
				YEARS = 10
			}
		}
		gdp_tgf_add_category_modifier_effect = { MODIFIER = outside_partners }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = paranoid factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1031.b
		house = {
			add_unity_value = {
				value = minor_unity_gain
				character = root
				desc = gdp_tgf_kept_in_family_unity_desc
			}
		}
		gdp_tgf_add_category_modifier_effect = { MODIFIER = kept_in_family }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade good category root's company is renowned in but no longer trades
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_lapsed_category_trigger = {
	has_trade_good_category_xp_track = yes
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_lapsed_cat_temp
	root = {
		gdp_tgf_renowned_in_category_trigger = { CATEGORY = scope:tgf_lapsed_cat_temp }
		NOT = {
			any_trade_category_traded_in = { this = scope:tgf_lapsed_cat_temp }
		}
	}
}

# Keeping Your Hand In
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1033 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1033.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1033.desc
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = study }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_left
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_good_category = { gdp_tgf_lapsed_category_trigger = yes }
	}

			limit = { gdp_tgf_lapsed_category_trigger = yes }

				is_commission_route = no
				any_traded_good = { always = yes }
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_current_category }
			}
		}

			years = 10
		}

		}

			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = gdp_tgf_sold_expertise_xp_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = paranoid factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1033.c
		add_stress = minor_stress_loss
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_current_category

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = diligent factor = 0 }
		}

	}
}

# this = trade route with a pilgrim in a market's local pool, carrying a good root is still fledgling in
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_itinerary_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_itinerary_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_broker_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_itinerary_route_temp }
	}
	any_traded_good = {
		trade_good_need_category = { save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_itinerary_cat_temp }
		root = {
			gdp_tgf_fledgling_in_category_trigger = { CATEGORY = scope:tgf_itinerary_cat_temp }
		}
	}
}

scripted_effect gdp_tgf_create_itinerary_effect = {
	gdp_tgf_roll_artifact_rarity_effect = { RARITY = random }
	create_artifact = {
		name = gdp_tgf_itinerary_artifact
		description = gdp_tgf_itinerary_artifact_desc
		type = library_item
		visuals = book
		modifier = gdp_tgf_itinerary_artifact_modifier
		quality = scope:quality
		wealth = scope:wealth
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	gdp_tgf_add_artifact_trade_modifiers_effect = yes
}

# The Itinerary
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1034 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1034.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1034.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = reading
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_pilgrim
		animation = holding_staff
		camera = camera_event_very_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			lowest_traded_good_category_xp_level < gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = gdp_tgf_had_event_1034 }
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_itinerary_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		add_character_flag = gdp_tgf_had_event_1034
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_itinerary_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_broker_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					trade_good_need_category = { save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_itinerary_cat_temp }
					root = { gdp_tgf_fledgling_in_category_trigger = { CATEGORY = scope:tgf_itinerary_cat_temp } }
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
				is_healthy = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_pilgrim
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_pilgrim
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1034.a
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:tgf_pilgrim
			gold = minor_gold_value
		}
		gdp_tgf_create_itinerary_effect = yes
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_major_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1034.b
		gdp_tgf_create_itinerary_effect = yes
		stress_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1034.c
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_miniscule_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = curious factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = character, a guest or local commoner fit to dine with root
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_dinner_guest_trigger = {
	NOT = { this = root }
	is_ai = yes
	is_physically_able_adult = yes
}

# Talking Shop
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1035 = {
	type = character_event

	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1035.desc
	theme = merchant

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = merchant_gesturing
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_guest
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_horses }
			animation = standing_horse
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_cattle }
			animation = holding_yoke
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_wool }
			animation = shepherd_with_sheep
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { OR = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_wine scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_beer } }
			animation = drink_goblet
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_meat }
			animation = holding_salami
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { OR = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_cheese scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_tea scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_sugar scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_rice scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_fruit scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_honey } }
			animation = serving_tray
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { OR = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_salt scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_spices scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_grain scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_millet } }
			animation = holding_scales
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_incense }
			animation = thurible
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_medicaments }
			animation = physician
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_oil }
			animation = lantern
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_books_paper }
			animation = holding_scrolls
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { OR = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_gold scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_silver scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_copper scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_fish } }
			animation = merchant_box
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_furs }
			animation = hunting_carcass_start
		}
		animation = pondering
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		OR = {
			any_pool_guest = { gdp_tgf_dinner_guest_trigger = yes }
			any_foreign_court_guest = { gdp_tgf_dinner_guest_trigger = yes }
			location = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_guest_province_temp
				any_pool_character = {
					province = scope:tgf_guest_province_temp
					is_healthy = yes
					gdp_tgf_dinner_guest_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		gdp_tgf_setup_good_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { any_pool_guest = { gdp_tgf_dinner_guest_trigger = yes } }
			random_pool_guest = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_dinner_guest_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = tgf_guest
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { any_foreign_court_guest = { gdp_tgf_dinner_guest_trigger = yes } }
			random_foreign_court_guest = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_dinner_guest_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = tgf_guest
			}
		}
		else = {
			location = {
				save_scope_as = tgf_guest_province
				random_pool_character = {
					province = scope:tgf_guest_province
					limit = {
						gdp_tgf_dinner_guest_trigger = yes
						is_healthy = yes
					}
					save_scope_as = tgf_guest
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1035.a
		trigger = {
			is_ai = no
			scope:tgf_guest = { short_term_gold >= miniscule_gold_value }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }
		scope:tgf_guest = {
			pay_short_term_gold = {
				target = root
				gold = miniscule_gold_value
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1035.b
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = buy_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = -0.003 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1035.c
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade route with a trader that carries a packable good
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_packing_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	any_traded_good = { gdp_tgf_not_livestock_good_trigger = yes }
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ buys a packable good and the local pool holds an adult to seal it
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_seal_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_seal_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			gdp_tgf_not_livestock_good_trigger = yes
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_seal_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_seal_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_seal_good_temp
				}
			}
		}
	}
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_seal_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_seal_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			is_physically_able_adult = yes
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_seal_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_seal_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_seal_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_seal_route_temp }
	}
}

# The Market Seal
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1037 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1037.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1037.desc

		animation = arguing
		camera = camera_event_left_to_the_left

		character = scope:tgf_sealer
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = {
				scope:tgf_source_province = {
					OR = {
						terrain = desert
						terrain = desert_mountains
						terrain = drylands
						terrain = oasis
						arid_region_trigger = yes
						AND = {
							tropical_seasons_region_trigger = yes
							has_winter_trigger = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			gui = event_window_widget_vfx_heat_wave
			container = background_shader_vfx_container
		}
		widget = {
			gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}

		is_available_allow_travelling = yes

	}

			limit = { gdp_tgf_seal_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}

							trade_market = scope:tgf_market

					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
				is_healthy = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_sealer
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_sealer
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1037.a
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			add_trade_route_modifier = {
				modifier = gdp_tgf_sealed_stock_wait_modifier
				years = 1
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1037.b

			target = scope:tgf_sealer
			gold = miniscule_gold_value
		}
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			gdp_tgf_add_tiered_route_modifier_effect = {
				MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_quick_seal
				YEARS = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1037.c
		if = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_root_renowned_in_event_category_trigger = yes }
			add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		}
		else = {
			gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
				CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
				VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
			}
		}

			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

# The Broker
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1038 = {
	type = character_event

		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_broker
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1038.a
		add_courtier = scope:tgf_broker
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = -0.01
				years = 10
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_broker_reach_renowned_modifier } }
			if = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_root_renowned_in_event_category_trigger = yes }
				remove_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_broker_reach_modifier
				add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_broker_reach_renowned_modifier
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_broker_reach_modifier } }
				add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_broker_reach_modifier
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = paranoid factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1038.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = lifestyle_merchant }
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = lifestyle_merchant
				track = caravaneer
				value = lifestyle_merchant_xp_gain_minor_value
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

		camera = camera_event_right_forward

		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

		modifier = {
			factor = 4
			NOT = { exists = player_heir }
		}
	}

			any_traded_good = { always = yes }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				is_commission_route = no
				has_trader = yes

					NOT = { this = root }
				}

			age = 16

		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_trader
			PROVINCE = root.location
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1039.a

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1039.b
		add_courtier = scope:tgf_youth
		scope:tgf_route = {
			gdp_tgf_add_tiered_route_modifier_effect = {
				MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_trained_hand
				YEARS = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_trader = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100

		}
	}

	option = {

			has_trait = greedy
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		gdp_tgf_flush_with_money_trigger = yes
		any_courtier = { gdp_tgf_purse_servant_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		gdp_tgf_setup_good_effect = yes
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_purse_servant_trigger = yes }
			weight = {
				base = 1
				modifier = {
					add = 4
					has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_servant
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1044.a
		remove_short_term_gold = miniscule_gold_value
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1044.b
		trigger = { short_term_gold >= medium_gold_value }
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value
		add_prestige = major_prestige_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1044.c
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = shy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = character who trades alcohol under a faith that forbids it
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_forbidden_alcohol_trigger = {
	faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = alcohol_forbidden }
	any_trade_route = {
		is_commission_route = no
		any_traded_good = {
			any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_alcohol }
		}
	}
}

# Profit and Piety
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1045 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1045.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1045.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = study }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = page_flipping
		camera = camera_event_left
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		NOT = { has_trait = cynical }
		ai_zeal >= 1
		OR = {
			has_trait = zealous
			has_trait = humble
			ai_zeal >= 50
			gdp_tgf_forbidden_alcohol_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_forbidden_alcohol_trigger = yes }
			random_trade_route = {
				limit = {
					is_commission_route = no
					any_traded_good = {
						any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_alcohol }
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_route
			}
			scope:tgf_route = {
				random_traded_good = {
					limit = {
						any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_alcohol }
					}
					save_scope_as = tgf_good
				}
			}
			scope:tgf_good = {
				trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
			}
			gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		}
		else = {
			gdp_tgf_setup_good_effect = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1045.a
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		add_piety = major_piety_gain
		stress_impact = {
			greedy = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1045.b
		add_stress = minor_stress_loss
		add_piety = miniscule_piety_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = cynical factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1045.c
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		stress_impact = {
			zealous = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = zealous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ buys, in a county of the route trader's culture but not root's
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_kin_trader_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_route_buys_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	trade_market_owner.title_province.county = {
		culture = $ROUTE$.trader.culture
		NOT = { culture = root.culture }
	}
}

# this = trade route whose trader is at home in a market on it where root is not
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_kin_trader_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	trader ?= {
		NOT = { this = root }
		is_physically_able_adult = yes
	}
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_kin_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_kin_trader_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_kin_route_temp }
	}
}

# One of Their Own
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1046 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1046.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1046.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = donkey_standing_baggage
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_kin_trader_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_kin_trader_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_kin_trader_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_kin_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_kin_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = {
						save_scope_as = tgf_source_county
						culture = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_culture }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_trader
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1046.a
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_trader = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1046.b
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:tgf_trader
			gold = miniscule_gold_value
		}
		scope:tgf_trader = {
			add_stewardship_skill = 1
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1046.c
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		scope:tgf_source_culture = {
			change_cultural_acceptance = {
				target = root.culture
				value = minor_cultural_acceptance_gain
				desc = cultural_acceptance_gain_event
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ sells a good and an AI rival trades too
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_flood_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_route_sells_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	any_potential_trade_rival_in_market = {
		target = root
		gdp_tgf_auction_rival_trigger = yes
	}
}

# this = trade route with a trader. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_flood_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_flood_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_flood_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_flood_route_temp }
	}
}

# Flooded Market
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1047 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1047.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1047.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = market_crowd }

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = arguing
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_rival
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:tgf_good }
			scripted_animation = gdp_tgf_good_animation
		}
		animation = merchant_box
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 15 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_flood_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_flood_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_flood_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_flood_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_flood_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			random_potential_trade_rival_in_market = {
				target = root
				limit = { gdp_tgf_auction_rival_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = tgf_rival
			}
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_rival = {
			random_held_title = {
				limit = { gdp_merchant_company_title_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = tgf_rival_company
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_trader
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1047.a
		trigger = { short_term_gold >= medium_gold_value }
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.03 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		gdp_tgf_add_tiered_character_modifier_effect = {
			MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_price_war
			YEARS = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1047.b
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = -0.005
				years = 10
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			impatient = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade good a book could be packed in
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_manuscript_good_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_not_livestock_good_trigger = yes
	NOT = { any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_alcohol } }
	NOR = {
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_oil
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_honey
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_books_paper
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_stone
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_wood
	}
}

# this = trade market far from root's domicile with a stranger holder, where $ROUTE$ buys a packable good
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_manuscript_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_manuscript_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	exists = root.domicile.domicile_location
	gdp_tgf_stranger_holder_market_trigger = yes
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		squared_distance = {
			target = root.domicile.domicile_location
			value > squared_distance_medium
		}
	}
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			gdp_tgf_manuscript_good_trigger = yes
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_manuscript_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_manuscript_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_manuscript_good_temp
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route with a trader. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_manuscript_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_manuscript_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_manuscript_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_manuscript_route_temp }
	}
}

			base = 100

		}

		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1048.b
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
		stress_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1048.c
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {

# this = pool character, an elder of root's culture

	culture = root.culture
	is_physically_able_adult = yes
	age >= 35
}

# this = market where $ROUTE$ sells, in another culture's county whose pool holds an elder of root's culture
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_diaspora_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_route_sells_here_trigger = { ROUTE = $ROUTE$ }
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		county = {
			NOT = { culture = root.culture }
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_diaspora_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_diaspora_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			gdp_tgf_diaspora_elder_trigger = yes
		}
	}
}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		exists = house
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_diaspora_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_diaspora_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_diaspora_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_diaspora_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_diaspora_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = {
						save_scope_as = tgf_source_county
						culture = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_culture }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ordered_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_diaspora_elder_trigger = yes
				is_healthy = yes
			}
			order_by = age
			save_scope_as = tgf_elder
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_elder
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1049.a
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_source_culture = {
			change_cultural_acceptance = {
				target = root.culture
				value = minor_cultural_acceptance_gain
				desc = cultural_acceptance_gain_event
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_elder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = -0.01
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1049.b
		add_courtier = scope:tgf_elder
		scope:tgf_elder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = shy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1049.c
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = compassionate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = pool character, a smuggler worth hiring
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_smuggler_candidate_trigger = {
	has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
	is_physically_able_adult = yes
	intrigue >= 10
}

# this = stranger's market where $ROUTE$ buys a non-livestock good and the pool holds a sly commoner
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_smuggler_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_smuggler_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	gdp_tgf_stranger_holder_market_trigger = yes
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			gdp_tgf_not_livestock_good_trigger = yes
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_smuggler_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_smuggler_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_smuggler_good_temp
				}
			}
		}
	}
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_smuggler_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_smuggler_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			gdp_tgf_smuggler_candidate_trigger = yes
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route with a trader. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_smuggler_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_smuggler_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_smuggler_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_smuggler_route_temp }
	}
}

# The Salt Road
gdp_trade_good_flavor.1050 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1050.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1050.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = trade_deal
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_smuggler
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_smuggler_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_smuggler_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_smuggler_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_not_livestock_good_trigger = yes
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_smuggler_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_smuggler_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		ordered_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_smuggler_candidate_trigger = yes
				is_healthy = yes
			}
			order_by = intrigue
			save_scope_as = tgf_smuggler
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_smuggler
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1050.a
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		random = {
			chance = 25
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1050.a.toast
				right_icon = scope:tgf_market_holder
				add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
				scope:tgf_market_holder = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = root
						modifier = annoyed_opinion
						opinion = -15
					}
				}
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1050.b
		scope:tgf_smuggler = {
			death = {
				death_reason = death_chopped_to_pieces
				killer = scope:tgf_market_holder
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_hook = scope:tgf_market_holder }
			}
			add_hook = {
				type = favor_hook
				target = scope:tgf_market_holder
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.1050.c
		add_courtier = scope:tgf_smuggler
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = paranoid factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

	type = character_event

	}
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = farmland }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_horses }

			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_cattle }

		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { OR = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_rice scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_fruit scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_sugar } }
			animation = serving_tray

		triggered_animation = {

		}
		animation = happiness
		camera = camera_event_left

		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
