			gdp_tgf_category_buy_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_farmed_goods }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_category_buy_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_farmed_goods }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_category_buy_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						CATEGORY = trade_category_farmed_goods
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_farmed_goods }
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_catbuy_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_catbuy_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = {
						save_scope_as = tgf_source_county
						culture = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_culture }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2001.a
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.03 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { NOT = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden } }
			text = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2001.b
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden }
			text = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2001.b.livestock
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_massive_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

			NOT = { has_character_flag = gdp_tgf_kept_haul_curio }
		}
		add_character_flag = gdp_tgf_kept_haul_curio
		gdp_tgf_create_study_curio_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2002.c
		scope:tgf_source_county = {

				limit = { gdp_tgf_county_held_by_root_or_ai_trigger = yes }

					MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_plentiful_stalls

				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {

				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {

	}
}

# A Rich Vein

		}
		name = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_amber }
			text = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2003.a.amber
		}
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 12
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_source_county = {
			if = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_county_held_by_root_or_ai_trigger = yes }
				gdp_tgf_add_tiered_county_modifier_effect = {
					MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_vein_share
					YEARS = 12
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = content factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2003.b
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = ambitious factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# Worth Its Secret
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2004 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2004.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_ceramics }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2004.desc.ceramics
			}
			desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2004.desc
		}
		desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2004.desc.outro
	}
	theme = trade
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_base_metals
				scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_precious_metals
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_jewelry
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_glass
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_smithy_workshop
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_leather
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_furs
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_dyes
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_tannery
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_wood
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_lacquerware
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_carpentry_workshop
	}
	override_background = { reference = gdp_artisan_street }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = trade_deal
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:tgf_good }
			scripted_animation = gdp_tgf_good_animation
		}
		animation = merchant_box
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			has_trader = yes
			gdp_tgf_category_buy_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_artisanal_goods }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				has_trader = yes
				gdp_tgf_category_buy_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_artisanal_goods }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_category_buy_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						CATEGORY = trade_category_artisanal_goods
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_artisanal_goods }
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_catbuy_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_catbuy_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_trader
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2004.a
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = content factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2004.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = ambitious factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# A Matter of Taste
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2005 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2005.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2005.desc
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = bp1_kitchen_western }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_tea }
			animation = drink
		}
		animation = eating
		camera = camera_event_left
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			gdp_tgf_category_sell_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_comforts }
		}
		NOR = {
			has_trait = temperate
			has_trait = inappetetic
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_category_sell_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_comforts }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_category_sell_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						CATEGORY = trade_category_comforts
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_comforts }
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_catsell_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_catsell_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = {
						save_scope_as = tgf_source_county
						culture = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_culture }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2005.a
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.03 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = content factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2005.b
		add_stress = medium_stress_loss
		hidden_effect = {
			change_current_weight = 25
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = temperate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade good worn as a cloak in Dress the Part
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_dress_good_trigger = {
	OR = {
		any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_clothes }
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_silk
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ sells a cloak good
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_dress_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_dress_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			gdp_tgf_dress_good_trigger = yes
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_dress_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_dress_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_dress_good_temp
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route that sells a cloak good in one of its markets
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_dress_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_dress_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_dress_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_dress_route_temp }
	}
}

scripted_effect gdp_tgf_dress_off_effect = {
	remove_character_flag = tgf_dress_furs
	remove_character_flag = tgf_dress_leather
	remove_character_flag = tgf_dress_textiles
	remove_character_flag = tgf_dress_silk
}

# Dress the Part
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2006 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2006.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2006.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = royal_wave
		camera = camera_event_left
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_dress_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_dress_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_dress_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_dress_good_trigger = yes
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_catsell_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_catsell_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_dress_off_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_furs }
			add_character_flag = tgf_dress_furs
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_leather }
			add_character_flag = tgf_dress_leather
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_silk }
			add_character_flag = tgf_dress_silk
		}
		else = {
			add_character_flag = tgf_dress_textiles
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = root
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2006.a
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		if = {
			limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_furs }
			custom_tooltip = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2006.a.tt_furs
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_leather }
			custom_tooltip = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2006.a.tt_leather
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_silk }
			custom_tooltip = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2006.a.tt_silk
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2006.a.tt_textiles
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2006.b
		hidden_effect = { gdp_tgf_dress_off_effect = yes }
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.015 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade route where a stranger's market buys $CATEGORY$ goods from it
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_category_sell_holder_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_catsell_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_category_sell_holder_market_trigger = {
			ROUTE = scope:tgf_catsell_route_temp
			CATEGORY = $CATEGORY$
		}
	}
}

# Face Value
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2007 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2007.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2007.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = gdp_smithy_workshop }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = merchant_box
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_market_holder
		animation = biting_coin
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = gdp_tgf_had_event_2007 }
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			gdp_tgf_category_sell_holder_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_precious_metals }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = tgf_coin_merchant
		add_character_flag = gdp_tgf_had_event_2007
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_category_sell_holder_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_precious_metals }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_category_sell_holder_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						CATEGORY = trade_category_precious_metals
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_precious_metals }
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_catsell_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_catsell_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2007.a

				type = sell_mult

				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = -0.01
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
		gdp_tgf_roll_artifact_rarity_effect = { RARITY = random }
		create_artifact = {
			name = gdp_tgf_coin_artifact
			description = gdp_tgf_coin_artifact_desc
			type = curio
			modifier = artifact_monthly_prestige_1_modifier
			visuals = medallion
			quality = scope:quality
			wealth = scope:wealth
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		}
		gdp_tgf_add_artifact_trade_modifiers_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2007.b
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -20
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}

			base = 100

		}
	}

	option = {

			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}

			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}

	}

# Adds Careful Handling for scope:tgf_category
scripted_effect gdp_tgf_add_careful_handling_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_alcohol }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_alcohol_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_comforts }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_comforts_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_cereals }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_cereals_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_meat }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_meat_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_beasts_of_burden_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_salt }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_salt_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_clothes }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_clothes_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_fabrics }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_fabrics_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_dyes }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_dyes_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_alum }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_alum_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_stone }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_stone_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_carpentry_supplies }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_carpentry_supplies_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_base_metals }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_base_metals_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_luxury_items }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_luxury_items_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_jewelry_materials }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_jewelry_materials_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_precious_metals }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_precious_metals_modifier
			years = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_medicaments }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_careful_handling_medicaments_modifier
			years = 25

	else_if = {

# Intact
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2008 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2008.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2008.desc
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = bp1_wine_cellar }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = happiness
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:tgf_good }
			scripted_animation = gdp_tgf_good_animation
		}
		animation = merchant_box
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			is_commission_route = no
			has_trader = yes
			any_traded_good = { is_breakable_trade_good_trigger = { TRADE_GOOD = this } }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				is_commission_route = no
				has_trader = yes
				any_traded_good = { is_breakable_trade_good_trigger = { TRADE_GOOD = this } }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = { is_breakable_trade_good_trigger = { TRADE_GOOD = this } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2008.a
		gdp_tgf_add_careful_handling_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2008.b
		scope:tgf_trader = {
			add_stewardship_skill = 2
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2008.c
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade good used to supply a war
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_war_good_trigger = {
	OR = {
		trade_good_need_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_base_metals }
		trade_good_need_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden }
		trade_good_need_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_carpentry_supplies }
	}
}

# this = trade market with a valid stranger holder at war, where $ROUTE$ sells a war-supply good
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_war_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_war_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	gdp_tgf_stranger_holder_market_trigger = yes
	trade_market_owner.holder = { is_at_war = yes }
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			gdp_tgf_war_good_trigger = yes
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_war_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_war_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_war_good_temp
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route where a warring stranger's market buys its war-supply goods
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_war_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_war_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_war_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_war_route_temp }
	}
}

# Other Men See Armies
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2009 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2009.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2009.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = army_camp }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = biting_coin
		camera = camera_event_left_to_the_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_market_holder
		animation = inspect_weapon
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_war_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_war_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_war_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_war_good_trigger = yes
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_war_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_war_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2009.a
		trigger = {
			is_ai = no
			scope:tgf_market_holder = { short_term_gold >= minor_gold_value }
		}
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			pay_short_term_gold = {
				target = root
				gold = minor_gold_value
			}
			gdp_tgf_add_tiered_character_modifier_effect = {
				MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_well_supplied_host
				YEARS = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = compassionate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2009.b
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			gdp_tgf_add_tiered_character_modifier_effect = {
				MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_well_supplied_host
				YEARS = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2009.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_hook = scope:tgf_market_holder }
		}
		add_hook = {
			type = favor_hook
			target = scope:tgf_market_holder
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			gdp_tgf_add_tiered_character_modifier_effect = {
				MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_well_supplied_host
				YEARS = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# The Guild Knocks
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2010 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2010.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2010.desc
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = gdp_artisan_street }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = {
				scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province ?= {
					OR = { arid_region_trigger = yes terrain = desert terrain = desert_mountains terrain = drylands terrain = oasis }
				}
			}
			animation = dromedary_holding
		}
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province ?= { terrain = steppe } }
			animation = standing_horse
		}
		animation = donkey_standing_baggage
		camera = camera_event_left
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			gdp_tgf_category_sell_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_guild_goods }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_category_sell_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_guild_goods }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_category_sell_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						CATEGORY = trade_category_guild_goods
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_guild_goods }
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_catsell_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_catsell_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = {
						save_scope_as = tgf_source_county
						culture = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_culture }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = root
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2010.a
		remove_short_term_gold = miniscule_gold_value
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = stubborn factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2010.b
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.005 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = content factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade good worn as a luxury
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_wearable_good_trigger = {
	OR = {
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_silk
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_jewelry
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_gems
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_amber
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_jade
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_ivory
	}
}

# this = trade market with a valid stranger holder, where $ROUTE$ sells a wearable luxury
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_worn_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_worn_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	gdp_tgf_stranger_holder_market_trigger = yes
	trade_market_owner.holder = {
		has_any_illness = no
	}
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			gdp_tgf_wearable_good_trigger = yes
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_worn_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_worn_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_worn_good_temp
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route where a stranger's market buys its wearable luxuries
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_worn_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_worn_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_worn_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_worn_route_temp }
	}
}

# this = the ruler now seen wearing scope:tgf_good
scripted_effect gdp_tgf_wear_finery_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_silk }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_silk_clad_modifier
			years = 5
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_bejeweled_modifier
			years = 5
		}
	}
}

			modifier = gdp_tgf_renowned_dealer_modifier
			years = 10
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2011.b
		scope:tgf_market_holder = { gdp_tgf_wear_finery_effect = yes }
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {

	}
}

# Sample the Goods
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2012 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2012.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2012.desc
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = bp1_wine_cellar }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = wedding_drunk
		camera = camera_torso
	}

	widgets = {
		widget = { gui = event_window_widget_vfx_background_double_vision_milder container = foreground_shader_vfx_container }
		widget = { gui = event_window_widget_vfx_left_character_double_vision_milder container = foreground_shader_vfx_container }
		widget = {
			gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		is_adult = yes
		stress >= 1
		NOT = { has_trait = drunkard }
		any_trade_route = {
			is_commission_route = no
			any_traded_good = {
				any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_alcohol }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				is_commission_route = no
				any_traded_good = {
					any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_alcohol }
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_alcohol }
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2012.a
		add_stress = massive_stress_loss
		random = {
			chance = 5
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2012.a.toast
				left_icon = root
				add_trait = drunkard
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = temperate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2012.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_miniscule_value
		}
		stress_impact = {
			temperate = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade good worth a master's showpiece
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_masterwork_good_trigger = {
	OR = {
		trade_good_need_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_luxury_items }
		trade_good_need_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_jewelry_materials }
		trade_good_need_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_precious_metals }
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ trades a masterwork-worthy good and the local pool holds an adult craftsman
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_masterwork_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_masterwork_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_masterwork_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_masterwork_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
			is_physically_able_adult = yes
		}
	}
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			gdp_tgf_masterwork_good_trigger = yes
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_masterwork_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				OR = {
					is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
						trade_market = scope:tgf_masterwork_market_temp
						trade_good_type = scope:tgf_masterwork_good_temp
					}
					is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
						trade_market = scope:tgf_masterwork_market_temp
						trade_good_type = scope:tgf_masterwork_good_temp
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route where a market trades its masterwork-worthy goods and has a craftsman in the local pool
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_masterwork_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_masterwork_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_masterwork_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_masterwork_route_temp }
	}
}

# A Masterwork Order
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2013 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2013.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2013.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_jewelry_materials
				scope:tgf_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_precious_metals
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_jewelry
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_smithy_workshop
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_leather
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_furs
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_dyes
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_tannery
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_wood
				scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_lacquerware
			}
		}
		reference = gdp_carpentry_workshop
	}
	override_background = { reference = gdp_artisan_street }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = pondering
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_crafter
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:tgf_good }
			scripted_animation = gdp_tgf_good_animation
		}
		animation = merchant_box
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			highest_traded_good_category_xp_level >= gdp_tgf_renowned_expertise_level_value
		}
	}

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = gdp_tgf_had_event_2013 }
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_masterwork_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		add_character_flag = gdp_tgf_had_event_2013
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_masterwork_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_masterwork_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_masterwork_good_trigger = yes
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_masterwork_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						OR = {
							is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
								trade_market = scope:tgf_market
								trade_good_type = scope:tgf_masterwork_good_temp
							}
							is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
								trade_market = scope:tgf_market
								trade_good_type = scope:tgf_masterwork_good_temp
							}
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_healthy = yes
				has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_crafter
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_crafter
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2013.a
		trigger = { short_term_gold >= medium_gold_value }
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:tgf_crafter
			gold = medium_gold_value
		}
		save_scope_as = tgf_showpiece_merchant
		gdp_tgf_roll_artifact_rarity_effect = { RARITY = random }
		create_artifact = {
			name = gdp_tgf_showpiece_artifact
			description = gdp_tgf_showpiece_artifact_desc
			type = curio
			modifier = artifact_monthly_prestige_2_modifier
			visuals = cabinet_trinket
			quality = scope:quality
			wealth = scope:wealth
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		}
		gdp_tgf_add_artifact_trade_modifiers_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2013.b
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade route with a trader that sells a food good in a market with a county
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_dish_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	gdp_tgf_category_sell_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_food }
}

# Their Dish, Our Goods
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2014 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2014.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2014.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = gdp_artisan_street_crowd }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = holding_salami
		camera = camera_event_right
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = eating
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_dish_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_dish_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_category_sell_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						CATEGORY = trade_category_food
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					has_category = trade_good_category:trade_category_food
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_dish_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_dish_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_trader
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2014.a
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2014.b
		scope:tgf_route = {
			gdp_tgf_add_tiered_route_modifier_effect = {
				MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_kitchen_demand
				YEARS = 10
			}
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = market where $ROUTE$ buys a forest good and the pool holds someone to speak for the gatherers

		CATEGORY = trade_category_forest_goods

		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_gatherers_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_gatherers_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			is_physically_able_adult = yes
		}
	}
}

# Paid in Goods
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2015 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2015.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2015.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = wilderness }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = debating
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_spokesperson
		animation = arguing
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_gatherers_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_gatherers_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_gatherers_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_forest_goods }
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_gatherers_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_gatherers_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
				is_healthy = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_spokesperson
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_spokesperson
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2015.a
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		scope:tgf_source_county = {
			if = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_county_held_by_root_or_ai_trigger = yes }
				gdp_tgf_add_tiered_county_modifier_effect = {
					MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_fair_wages
					YEARS = 10
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_spokesperson = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2015.b
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_spokesperson = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = paranoid factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2015.c
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_source_county = {
			if = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_county_held_by_root_or_ai_trigger = yes }
				add_county_modifier = {
					modifier = gdp_tgf_underpaid_gatherers_modifier
					years = 10
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_spokesperson = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -20
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = generous factor = 0 }
			modifier = { has_trait = compassionate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade good that smells
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_smelly_good_trigger = {
	OR = {
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_fish
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_leather
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_dyes
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_cheese
		this = trade_good_type:trade_good_hemp
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ sells a smelly good and the local pool holds a buyer to complain
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_stink_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_stink_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			gdp_tgf_smelly_good_trigger = yes
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_stink_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
					trade_market = scope:tgf_stink_market_temp
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_stink_good_temp
				}
			}
		}
	}
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_stink_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_stink_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			is_physically_able_adult = yes
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_stink_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_stink_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_stink_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_stink_route_temp }
	}

		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2016.a
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.015 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { can_contract_disease_trigger = { DISEASE = ill } }
			random = {
				chance = 10
				contract_disease_effect = {
					DISEASE = ill
					TREATMENT_EVENT = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2016.b
		remove_short_term_gold = miniscule_gold_value
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = trade_good_category:trade_category_salt
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_massive_value
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = trade_good_category:trade_category_medicaments
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_massive_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2016.c
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# Seven Fat Years
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2017 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2017.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2017.desc
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = bedchamber }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = delirium
		camera = camera_event_left
	}

	widgets = {
		widget = { gui = event_window_widget_vfx_background_night_scene container = foreground_shader_vfx_container }
		widget = { gui = event_window_widget_vfx_left_character_night_scene container = foreground_shader_vfx_container }
		widget = {
			gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_category_buy_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_food } }
		NOT = {
			has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_seven_storehouses_modifier
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		add_character_flag = {
			flag = use_sickness_clothes
			days = 7
		}
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_category_buy_route_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_food } }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_category_buy_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						CATEGORY = trade_category_food
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					any_trade_category = { this = trade_good_category:trade_category_food }
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_storehouse_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
							trade_market = scope:tgf_market
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_storehouse_good_temp
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2017.a
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_seven_storehouses_modifier
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2017.b
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.007 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 7
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = patient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2017.c
		add_stress = medium_stress_loss
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = ambitious factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		remove_character_flag = use_sickness_clothes
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = pool character, a cattle raider
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_raider_candidate_trigger = {
	has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
	is_physically_able_adult = yes
	prowess >= medium_skill_rating
}

# this = trade market with a stranger holder, where $ROUTE$ trades livestock and the local pool holds a raider
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_raider_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_raider_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	gdp_tgf_stranger_holder_market_trigger = yes
	$ROUTE$ = {
		any_traded_good = {
			gdp_tgf_not_livestock_good_trigger = no
			save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_raider_good_temp
			$ROUTE$ = {
				OR = {
					is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
						trade_market = scope:tgf_raider_market_temp
						trade_good_type = scope:tgf_raider_good_temp
					}
					is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
						trade_market = scope:tgf_raider_market_temp
						trade_good_type = scope:tgf_raider_good_temp
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_raider_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_raider_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			gdp_tgf_raider_candidate_trigger = yes
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route with a trader. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_raider_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_raider_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_raider_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_raider_route_temp }
	}
}

# Somebody's Cattle
gdp_trade_good_flavor.2018 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2018.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2018.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = farmland }

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = merchant_gesturing
		camera = camera_event_very_left_fov35
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_raider
		animation = donkey_standing_baggage
		camera = camera_event_right_forward
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 25 }

	trigger = {
		is_available = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_raider_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_raider_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_raider_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
			random_traded_good = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_not_livestock_good_trigger = no
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_raider_good_temp
					scope:tgf_route = {
						OR = {
							is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
								trade_market = scope:tgf_market
								trade_good_type = scope:tgf_raider_good_temp
							}
							is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
								trade_market = scope:tgf_market
								trade_good_type = scope:tgf_raider_good_temp
							}
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_good
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		ordered_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_raider_candidate_trigger = yes
				is_healthy = yes
			}
			order_by = prowess
			save_scope_as = tgf_raider
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2018.a
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = buy_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = -0.03 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_major_value
		}
		random = {
			chance = 25
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2018.a.toast
				right_icon = scope:tgf_market_holder
				add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
				scope:tgf_market_holder = {
					progress_towards_rival_effect = {
						REASON = rival_tgf_bought_stolen_herd
						CHARACTER = root
						OPINION = default_rival_opinion
					}
				}
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			just = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = just factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2018.b
		scope:tgf_raider = {
			death = {
				death_reason = death_chopped_to_pieces
				killer = scope:tgf_market_holder
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_hook = scope:tgf_market_holder }
			}
			add_hook = {
				type = favor_hook
				target = scope:tgf_market_holder
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.2018.c
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		stress_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ sells alcohol to a stranger holder whose faith forbids it
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_dry_court_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_category_sell_holder_market_trigger = {
		ROUTE = $ROUTE$
		CATEGORY = trade_category_alcohol
	}
	trade_market_owner.holder.faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = alcohol_forbidden }
}

# this = trade route with a trader. Expects root = the merchant
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_dry_court_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_dry_court_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_dry_court_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_dry_court_route_temp }
	}
}

			gdp_tgf_good_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_cheese }
		}

	immediate = {

		random_courtier = {
			limit = { is_physically_able_adult = yes }
			weight = {
				base = 1

					add = 4
					is_knight = yes
				}
			}

		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_cheese = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}

			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_massive_value
		}
		gdp_tgf_roll_artifact_rarity_effect = { RARITY = random }
		create_artifact = {
			name = fp2_cheese_artifact
			description = fp2_cheese_artifact_desc
			modifier = artifact_health_gain_1_modifier
			modifier = artifact_attraction_opinion_negative_1_modifier
			type = miscellaneous
			visuals = pouch
			quality = scope:quality
			wealth = scope:wealth
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		}
		gdp_tgf_add_artifact_trade_modifiers_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:newly_created_artifact = {
				flag_as_trash_artifact = yes
				set_variable = has_cheese_artifact
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = temperate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3001.b
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = gluttonous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_copper = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3002.a
		random_list = {
			70 = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3002.a.toast_good
					right_icon = scope:tgf_market_holder
					scope:tgf_market = {
						add_price_modifier_for_character = {
							type = sell_mult
							character = root
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
							value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
							years = 10
						}
					}
				}
			}
			30 = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3002.a.toast
					right_icon = scope:tgf_market_holder
					scope:tgf_market = {
						add_price_modifier_for_character = {
							type = sell_mult
							character = root
							trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
							value = -0.02
							years = 10
						}
					}
					scope:tgf_market_holder = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = root
							modifier = annoyed_opinion
							opinion = -20
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3002.b
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			target = scope:tgf_market_holder
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				min = 10
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3002.b.toast_good
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3002.b.toast_good
					right_icon = scope:tgf_market_holder
					add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				min = 10
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3002.b.toast_bad
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3002.b.toast_bad
					right_icon = scope:tgf_market_holder
					scope:tgf_market_holder = {
						progress_towards_rival_effect = {
							REASON = rival_tgf_light_ingots
							CHARACTER = root
							OPINION = default_rival_opinion
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {

		}

	after = {

	}

# this = trade market whose local pool holds a poor adult commoner
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_debtor_market_trigger = {
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_debtor_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_debtor_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
			is_physically_able_adult = yes
			short_term_gold < 5
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route where a market has a destitute commoner in the local pool
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_debtor_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	gdp_tgf_good_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_spices }
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_debtor_market_trigger = yes
	}
}

# Rent in Pepper
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3003 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3003.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3003.desc
	theme = merchant

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_debtor
		animation = beg
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_debtor_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_debtor_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_debtor_market_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_healthy = yes
				has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
				short_term_gold < 5
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_debtor
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_spices = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3003.a
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 20
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = stubborn factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3003.b
		trigger = {
			is_ai = no
		}
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }
		scope:tgf_debtor = {
			pay_short_term_gold = {
				target = root
				gold = miniscule_gold_value
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -20
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = compassionate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3003.c
		add_courtier = scope:tgf_debtor
		scope:tgf_debtor = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 30
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# A Letter Full of Complaints
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3004 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3004.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3004.desc
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = terrain_scope }

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = mental_anguish
	}

	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { scope:tgf_weather = flag:rain }
			gui = event_window_widget_vfx_rain_storm
			container = foreground_shader_vfx_container
		}
		widget = {
			is_shown = { scope:tgf_weather = flag:snow }
			gui = event_window_widget_vfx_snowstorm
			container = foreground_shader_vfx_container
		}
		widget = {
			gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			has_trader = yes
			gdp_tgf_good_sell_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_flax }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				has_trader = yes
				gdp_tgf_good_sell_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_flax }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_good_sell_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						GOOD = trade_good_flax
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_flax = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		scope:tgf_trader.location ?= { save_scope_as = background_terrain_scope }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:background_terrain_scope } }
			scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province = { save_scope_as = background_terrain_scope }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:background_terrain_scope = { has_winter_trigger = yes } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = tgf_weather value = flag:snow }
		}
		else = {
			save_scope_value_as = { name = tgf_weather value = flag:rain }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3004.a
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = diligent factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3004.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_major_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

					}
				}

		}

		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes

			has_trader = yes
			gdp_tgf_good_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_fruit }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				has_trader = yes
				gdp_tgf_good_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_fruit }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_fruit = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3008.a
		remove_short_term_gold = miniscule_gold_value
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.005 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3008.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = trade_good_category:trade_category_alcohol
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_massive_value
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_alcohol_opportunist_modifier
			years = 15
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = temperate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# In the Blade
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3010 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3010.t
	desc = {
		desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3010.desc.intro
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					gdp_usury_condemns_loan_trigger = { BORROWER = scope:tgf_peasant }
				}
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3010.desc.condemned
			}
			desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3010.desc.permitted
		}
	}
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = farmland }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_peasant
		animation = beg
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			gdp_tgf_good_buy_pool_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_grain }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_good_buy_pool_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_grain }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_good_buy_pool_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						GOOD = trade_good_grain
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_healthy = yes
				has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_peasant
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_grain = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3010.a
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 25
			}
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_major_value
		}
		scope:tgf_peasant = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				gdp_usury_condemns_loan_trigger = { BORROWER = scope:tgf_peasant }
			}
			add_piety = minor_piety_loss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = zealous factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3010.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				gdp_usury_condemns_loan_trigger = { BORROWER = scope:tgf_peasant }
			}
			add_piety = miniscule_piety_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}
