# Polished or Not
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3012 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3012.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3012.desc
	theme = merchant
	override_background = { reference = farmland }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = donkey_standing_baggage
		camera = camera_event_table_left_2
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			has_trader = yes
			gdp_tgf_good_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_rice }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				has_trader = yes
				gdp_tgf_good_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_rice }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_rice_market_temp
					exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
					scope:tgf_route = {
						OR = {
							is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
								trade_market = scope:tgf_rice_market_temp
								trade_good_type = trade_good_type:trade_good_rice
							}
							is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
								trade_market = scope:tgf_rice_market_temp
								trade_good_type = trade_good_type:trade_good_rice
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trade_market_owner.title_province.county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
			}
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_rice = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3012.a
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = humble factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3012.b
		scope:tgf_source_county ?= {
			if = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_county_held_by_root_or_ai_trigger = yes }
				add_county_modifier = {
					modifier = gdp_tgf_cheap_rice_modifier
					years = 10
				}
			}
		}
		add_piety = minor_piety_gain
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3012.c
		add_prestige = miniscule_prestige_gain
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_miniscule_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ sells meat, in a county that keeps Lent or Ramadan
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_fast_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_good_sell_market_trigger = {
		ROUTE = $ROUTE$
		GOOD = trade_good_meat
	}
	trade_market_owner.title_province.county.faith = {
		OR = {
			religion = religion:christianity_religion
			religion = religion:islam_religion
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route with a trader and a market that keeps a meat fast
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_fast_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_fast_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_fast_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_fast_route_temp }
	}
}

# Before the Fast
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3013 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3013.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					scope:tgf_source_faith = { religion = religion:islam_religion }
				}
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3013.desc_ramadan
			}
			desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3013.desc
		}
	}
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = holding_salami
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = eating_salami
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_fast_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_fast_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_fast_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = {
						save_scope_as = tgf_source_county
						faith = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_faith }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_meat = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_trader
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3013.a
		trigger = { faith.religion = scope:tgf_source_faith.religion }
		reason = faith
		add_piety = minor_piety_gain
		add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_keeps_the_fast_modifier
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = cynical factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3013.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_trade_route = {
						is_commission_route = no
						any_traded_good = { this = trade_good_type:trade_good_salt }
					}
				}
			}
			remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = trade_good_category:trade_category_salt
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_massive_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3013.c
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = patient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade route that buys horses where a dealer waits, while root is still fledgling with beasts of burden
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_horse_dealer_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	gdp_tgf_good_buy_pool_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_horses }
	root = {
		gdp_tgf_fledgling_in_category_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_good_category:trade_category_beasts_of_burden }
	}
}

			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 20
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = trusting factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3014.b
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = had_warhorse_story
				any_owned_story = { type = story_cycle_martial_lifestyle_warhorse }
			}
		}
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:tgf_dealer
			gold = miniscule_gold_value
		}
		start_warhorse_story_cycle_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = craven factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3014.c
		add_courtier = scope:tgf_dealer
		scope:tgf_dealer = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 30
			}
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100

		}

# Fatten for Market
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3015 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3015.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3015.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = farmland }

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = merchant_gesturing
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_market_holder
		animation = biting_coin
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			has_trader = yes
			gdp_tgf_good_sell_holder_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_cattle }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				has_trader = yes
				gdp_tgf_good_sell_holder_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_cattle }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_good_sell_holder_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						GOOD = trade_good_cattle
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_cattle = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3015.a
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:tgf_market_holder
			gold = miniscule_gold_value
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 20
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_source_county = {
			if = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_county_held_by_root_or_ai_trigger = yes }
				gdp_tgf_add_tiered_county_modifier_effect = {
					MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_well_grazed_meadows
					YEARS = 20
				}
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			gdp_tgf_add_tiered_route_modifier_effect = {
				MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_heavier_loads
				YEARS = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3015.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = patient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = market where $ROUTE$ sells leather to a holder whose available chaplain follows a parchment-scripture faith
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_chaplain_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_good_sell_holder_market_trigger = {
		ROUTE = $ROUTE$
		GOOD = trade_good_leather
	}
	trade_market_owner.holder = {
		cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			OR = {
				religion = { is_in_family = rf_abrahamic }
				religion = religion:zoroastrianism_religion
			}
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route that sells leather to a holder whose court chaplain can write
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_chaplain_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_chaplain_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_chaplain_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_chaplain_route_temp }
	}
}

# Calfskin for the Scribes
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3016 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3016.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3016.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = gdp_tannery }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = merchant_gesturing
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_chaplain
		animation = reading
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		NOR = {
			religion = religion:hinduism_religion
			religion = religion:jainism_religion
		}
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_chaplain_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_chaplain_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_chaplain_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder
					cp:councillor_court_chaplain = { save_scope_as = tgf_chaplain }
				}
			}
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_leather = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3016.a
		if = {
			limit = { faith = scope:tgf_chaplain.faith }
			add_piety = medium_piety_gain
		}
		else = {
			add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = trade_good_category:trade_category_books
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3016.b
		add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_parchment_supplier_modifier
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = trade_good_category:trade_category_books
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = cynical factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3016.c
		add_trade_good_price_modifier = {
			type = sell_mult
			trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
			value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
			years = 10
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = zealous factor = 0 }
		}

		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}

# this = province with a church holding held by an AI Christian other than root
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_abbey_province_trigger = {
	has_holding_type = church_holding
	barony.holder ?= {
		NOT = { this = root }
		is_ai = yes
		is_physically_able_adult = yes
		religion = religion:christianity_religion
	}
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ buys wool in a county with a Christian church holding
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_abbey_market_trigger = {
	gdp_tgf_good_buy_market_trigger = {
		ROUTE = $ROUTE$
		GOOD = trade_good_wool
	}
	trade_market_owner.title_province.county = {
		any_county_province = { gdp_tgf_abbey_province_trigger = yes }
	}
}

# this = trade route that buys wool in a county with a Christian church holding
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_abbey_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_goodbuy_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_abbey_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_goodbuy_route_temp }
	}
}

# Buying the Clip
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3017 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3017.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3017.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = hills }

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = merchant_gesturing
		camera = camera_event_left_away_3_4_small
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_abbot
		animation = shepherd_with_sheep
		camera = camera_event_very_right_fov50
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			has_trader = yes
			gdp_tgf_abbey_route_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				has_trader = yes
				gdp_tgf_abbey_route_trigger = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_abbey_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = {
						random_county_province = {
							limit = { gdp_tgf_abbey_province_trigger = yes }
							save_scope_as = tgf_abbey_province
							barony = { save_scope_as = tgf_abbey }
							barony.holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_abbot }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_wool = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3017.a
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:tgf_abbot
			gold = minor_gold_value
		}
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = 0.05
				years = 3
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = buy_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = -0.03 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 15
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3017.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = patient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

		container = "custom_widgets_container"

		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes

		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_good_sell_pool_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_cotton }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {

				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_good_sell_pool_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route

					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_healthy = yes
				has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_buyer
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_cotton = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_buyer
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3018.a
		add_prestige = miniscule_prestige_gain
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = honest factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3018.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_major_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = deceitful factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {

	}
}

			limit = {
				has_trader = yes
				gdp_tgf_mast_route_trigger = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_mast_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_wood = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_market_holder
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3020.a
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_tall_mast_modifier
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3020.b
		scope:tgf_route = {
			gdp_tgf_add_tiered_route_modifier_effect = {
				MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_our_timber
				YEARS = 15
			}
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = patient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3020.c
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.05 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 15
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = hashish eater other than root
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_hashish_eater_trigger = {
	has_trait = hashishiyah
	this != root
	is_ai = yes
	is_healthy = yes
}

# this = trade market where $ROUTE$ sells hemp or medicaments to a hemp-using culture or a hashish eater
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_hemp_custom_market_trigger = {
	OR = {
		gdp_tgf_good_sell_market_trigger = {
			ROUTE = $ROUTE$
			GOOD = trade_good_hemp
		}
		gdp_tgf_good_sell_market_trigger = {
			ROUTE = $ROUTE$
			GOOD = trade_good_medicaments
		}
	}
	OR = {
		trade_market_owner.title_province.county.culture = {
			OR = {
				has_building_gfx = steppe_building_gfx
				has_building_gfx = indian_building_gfx
				AND = {
					current_year >= 1100
					OR = {
						has_building_gfx = mena_building_gfx
						has_building_gfx = iranian_building_gfx
					}
				}
			}
		}
		any_character_in_market = { gdp_tgf_hashish_eater_trigger = yes }
	}
}

# this = trade route that sells hemp or medicaments in such a market
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_hemp_custom_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_goodsell_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_hemp_custom_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_goodsell_route_temp }
	}
}

# The Tent of Smoke
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021 = {
	type = character_event
	title = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:tgf_hemp_custom = flag:bhang }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021.t_bhang
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:tgf_hemp_custom = flag:hashish }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021.t_hashish
			}
			desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021.t
		}
	}
	desc = {
		desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021.desc.intro
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:tgf_hemp_custom = flag:bhang }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021.desc_bhang
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:tgf_hemp_custom = flag:hashish }
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021.desc_hashish
			}
			desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021.desc
		}
	}
	theme = trade
	override_background = {
		trigger = { scope:tgf_hemp_custom = flag:smoke }
		reference = mpo_tent_interior_mongol
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = { scope:tgf_hemp_custom = flag:bhang }
		reference = bp2_indian_garden
	}
	override_background = {
		trigger = { scope:tgf_hemp_custom = flag:hashish }
		reference = bp2_tavern_mena
	}

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_hemp_custom = flag:bhang }
			animation = drink_goblet
		}
		animation = delirium
		camera = camera_event_very_left
	}

	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_hemp_host
		animation = laugh
	}

	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { scope:tgf_hemp_custom = flag:smoke }
			gui = event_window_widget_vfx_heavy_smoke
			container = foreground_shader_vfx_container
		}
		widget = {
			gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_hemp_custom_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_hemp_custom_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_hemp_custom_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					county = {
						save_scope_as = tgf_source_county
						culture = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_culture }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:tgf_source_culture = { has_building_gfx = steppe_building_gfx }
			}
			save_scope_value_as = {
				name = tgf_hemp_custom
				value = flag:smoke
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:tgf_source_culture = { has_building_gfx = indian_building_gfx }
			}
			save_scope_value_as = {
				name = tgf_hemp_custom
				value = flag:bhang
			}
		}
		else = {
			save_scope_value_as = {
				name = tgf_hemp_custom
				value = flag:hashish
			}
			scope:tgf_market = {
				random_character_in_market = {
					limit = { gdp_tgf_hashish_eater_trigger = yes }
					save_scope_as = tgf_hashish_eater
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:tgf_market = {
					gdp_tgf_good_sell_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						GOOD = trade_good_hemp
					}
				}
			}
			trade_good_type:trade_good_hemp = { save_scope_as = tgf_good }
		}
		else = {
			trade_good_type:trade_good_medicaments = { save_scope_as = tgf_good }
		}
		scope:tgf_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:tgf_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_hemp }
			trade_good_type:trade_good_medicaments = { save_scope_as = tgf_cross_good }
		}
		else = {
			trade_good_type:trade_good_hemp = { save_scope_as = tgf_cross_good }
		}
		scope:tgf_cross_good = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_cross_category }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:tgf_hashish_eater }
			scope:tgf_hashish_eater = { save_scope_as = tgf_hemp_host }
		}
		else = {
			random_pool_character = {
				province = scope:tgf_source_county.title_province
				limit = {
					is_healthy = yes
					is_physically_able_adult = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_hemp_host
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:tgf_hemp_host }
			}
			create_character = {
				age = { 25 55 }
				gender_female_chance = 50
				dynasty = none
				culture = scope:tgf_source_culture
				faith = scope:tgf_source_county.faith
				location = scope:tgf_source_county.title_province
				after_creation = {
					add_character_flag = created
					trigger_race_giving_no_gene_effect = yes # Warcraft
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_hemp_host
			}
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_hemp_custom = flag:smoke }
			text = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021.a
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_hemp_custom = flag:bhang }
			text = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021.a_bhang
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { scope:tgf_hemp_custom = flag:hashish }
			text = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021.a_hashish
		}
		add_stress = medium_stress_loss
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.05 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_cross_good
				value = { value = 0.05 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_trait = hashishiyah }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 5
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021.a.toast_habit
					left_icon = root
					add_trait = hashishiyah
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = temperate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3021.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_cross_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_major_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = gregarious factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
		scope:tgf_hemp_host = { silent_disappearance_ai_if_created_effect = yes }
	}
}

# Creates the weapon scope:tgf_smith forges for scope:tgf_smith_patron
# $TYPE$ is both the artifact type and the visuals
scripted_effect gdp_tgf_create_smith_weapon_effect = {
	create_artifact = {
		name = gdp_tgf_smith_weapon_$TYPE$
		description = gdp_tgf_smith_weapon_desc
		creator = scope:tgf_smith
		type = $TYPE$
		visuals = $TYPE$
		modifier = artifact_prowess_1_modifier
		quality = scope:quality
		wealth = scope:wealth
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
}

# A Blade From My Best
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3022 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3022.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3022.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = gdp_smithy_workshop }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = interested
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_smith
		animation = merchant_box
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widgets = {
		widget = { gui = event_window_widget_vfx_heat_wave container = background_shader_vfx_container }
		widget = {
			gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = gdp_tgf_had_event_3022 }
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			gdp_tgf_good_buy_pool_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_iron }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = tgf_smith_patron
		add_character_flag = gdp_tgf_had_event_3022
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_good_buy_pool_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_iron }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_good_buy_pool_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						GOOD = trade_good_iron
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_healthy = yes
				has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
			}
			weight = {
				base = 1
				modifier = {
					add = martial
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_smith
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_iron = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = tgf_weapon_type
					value = flag:axe
				}
			}
			1 = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = tgf_weapon_type
					value = flag:dagger
				}
			}
			1 = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = tgf_weapon_type
					value = flag:hammer
				}
			}
			1 = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = tgf_weapon_type
					value = flag:mace
				}
			}
			1 = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = tgf_weapon_type
					value = flag:spear
				}
			}
			1 = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = tgf_weapon_type
					value = flag:sword
				}
			}
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3022.a
		trigger = { short_term_gold >= medium_gold_value }
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:tgf_smith
			gold = medium_gold_value
		}
		gdp_tgf_roll_artifact_rarity_effect = { RARITY = random }
		if = {
			limit = { scope:tgf_weapon_type = flag:axe }
			gdp_tgf_create_smith_weapon_effect = { TYPE = axe }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { scope:tgf_weapon_type = flag:dagger }
			gdp_tgf_create_smith_weapon_effect = { TYPE = dagger }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { scope:tgf_weapon_type = flag:hammer }
			gdp_tgf_create_smith_weapon_effect = { TYPE = hammer }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { scope:tgf_weapon_type = flag:mace }
			gdp_tgf_create_smith_weapon_effect = { TYPE = mace }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { scope:tgf_weapon_type = flag:spear }
			gdp_tgf_create_smith_weapon_effect = { TYPE = spear }
		}
		else = {
			gdp_tgf_create_smith_weapon_effect = { TYPE = sword }
		}
		gdp_tgf_add_artifact_trade_modifiers_effect = yes
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3022.b
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

					}

				is_physically_able_adult = yes

			CHAR = scope:tgf_lapidary

		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3023.a
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root

				value = { value = 0.02 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 20
			}

		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss

			base = 100

		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3023.b
		add_courtier = scope:tgf_lapidary
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3023.c
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = ambitious factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
		remove_variable = tgf_ailment
	}
}

# this = pool character skilled enough to be worth hiring as a jade connoisseur
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_connoisseur_candidate_trigger = {
	has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
	is_physically_able_adult = yes
	OR = {
		stewardship >= decent_skill_rating
		learning >= decent_skill_rating
	}
}

# this = trade market; $ROUTE$ buys or sells jade here and the local pool holds a skilled connoisseur
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_connoisseur_market_trigger = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_connoisseur_market_temp
	exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county
	$ROUTE$ = {
		OR = {
			is_buying_trade_good_in_market = {
				trade_market = scope:tgf_connoisseur_market_temp
				trade_good_type = trade_good_type:trade_good_jade
			}
			is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
				trade_market = scope:tgf_connoisseur_market_temp
				trade_good_type = trade_good_type:trade_good_jade
			}
		}
	}
	trade_market_owner.title_province = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_connoisseur_province_temp
		any_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_connoisseur_province_temp
			is_healthy = yes
			gdp_tgf_connoisseur_candidate_trigger = yes
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route that trades jade in a market whose pool holds a skilled connoisseur
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_connoisseur_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_connoisseur_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_connoisseur_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_connoisseur_route_temp }
	}
}

# The Gentleman's Stone
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3024 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3024.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3024.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = gdp_artisan_street }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = pondering
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_connoisseur
		animation = holding_scales
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = { gdp_tgf_connoisseur_route_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_connoisseur_route_trigger = yes }
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { gdp_tgf_connoisseur_market_trigger = { ROUTE = scope:tgf_route } }
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		ordered_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_connoisseur_candidate_trigger = yes
				is_healthy = yes
			}
			order_by = {
				value = stewardship
				add = learning
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_connoisseur
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_jade = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_connoisseur
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3024.a
		remove_short_term_gold = minor_gold_value
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:tgf_route = {
					is_selling_trade_good_in_market = {
						trade_market = scope:tgf_market
						trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
					}
				}
			}
			scope:tgf_market = {
				add_price_modifier_for_character = {
					type = sell_mult
					character = root
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
					value = { value = 0.03 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:tgf_market = {
				add_price_modifier_for_character = {
					type = buy_mult
					character = root
					trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
					value = { value = -0.03 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3024.b
		add_courtier = scope:tgf_connoisseur
		scope:tgf_connoisseur = {
			add_stewardship_skill = 2
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_minor_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = arrogant factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3024.c
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade route with a trader that carries ceramics across the sea
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_sea_ceramics_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	has_trader = yes
	gdp_tgf_good_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_ceramics }
	any_road_on_route = {
		any_road_province = { is_sea_province = yes }
	}
}

# Creates the Reading Stones scope:tgf_glassmaker grinds for this character
# $OWNER$ is merchant or lord and picks the name key
scripted_effect gdp_tgf_create_reading_stones_effect = {
	gdp_tgf_roll_artifact_rarity_effect = { RARITY = random }
	create_artifact = {
		name = gdp_tgf_reading_stones_artifact_$OWNER$
		description = gdp_tgf_reading_stones_artifact_desc
		creator = scope:tgf_glassmaker
		modifier = artifact_stewardship_1_modifier
		type = miscellaneous
		visuals = pocket_fancy_case
		quality = scope:quality
		wealth = scope:wealth
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = { set_variable = tgf_reading_stones }
}

				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_good_buy_pool_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route

					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder ?= { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_healthy = yes

			}

			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3027.a
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:tgf_glassmaker
			gold = minor_gold_value
		}

		gdp_tgf_add_artifact_trade_modifiers_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = greedy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {

		trigger = {
			scope:tgf_market = { gdp_tgf_stranger_holder_market_trigger = yes }
		}

			target = scope:tgf_glassmaker

		}

			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_healthy = yes
				has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
			}
			weight = {
				base = 1
				modifier = {
					add = learning
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_apothecary
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_medicaments = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3029.a
		scope:tgf_route = {
			gdp_tgf_add_tiered_route_modifier_effect = {
				MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_theriac_peddlers
				YEARS = 20
			}
			every_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = { exists = trade_market_owner.title_province.county }
				trade_market_owner.title_province.county = {
					if = {
						limit = { gdp_tgf_county_held_by_root_or_ai_trigger = yes }
						gdp_tgf_add_tiered_county_modifier_effect = {
							MODIFIER = gdp_tgf_theriac_supply
							YEARS = 20
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = lazy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3029.b
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.03 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 15
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = content factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3029.c
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_theriac_dose_modifier
			years = 20
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# No Paper for Charters
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3030 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3030.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3030.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = study }

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_trader
		animation = holding_scrolls
		camera = camera_event_very_right_pointing_very_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_market_holder
		animation = dismissal
		camera = camera_event_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			has_trader = yes
			gdp_tgf_good_sell_holder_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_books_paper }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				has_trader = yes
				gdp_tgf_good_sell_holder_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_books_paper }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			trader = { save_scope_as = tgf_trader }
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_good_sell_holder_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						GOOD = trade_good_books_paper
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_books_paper = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_leather = {
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_leather_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3030.a
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			target = scope:tgf_market_holder
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = 3.5
					min = -49
				}
				min = 5
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3030.a.success
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3030.a.success
					right_icon = scope:tgf_market_holder
					add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_chancery_stationer_modifier
					add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = {
					value = scope:duel_value
					multiplier = -3.5
					min = -49
				}
				min = 5
				desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3030.a.failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3030.a.failure
					right_icon = scope:tgf_market_holder
					add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
					scope:tgf_market_holder = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = root
							modifier = annoyed_opinion
							opinion = -10
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = shy factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3030.b
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = trade_good_type:trade_good_leather
				value = { value = 0.03 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 15
			}
		}
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_leather_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_major_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = ambitious factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3030.c
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_medium_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = impatient factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = trade market in a county of Frankish or Germanic culture, where brewers paid for gruit
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_gruit_market_trigger = {
	trade_market_owner.title_province.county.culture = {
		OR = {
			has_cultural_pillar = heritage_frankish
			has_cultural_pillar = heritage_west_germanic
			has_cultural_pillar = heritage_central_germanic
			has_cultural_pillar = heritage_north_germanic
		}
	}
}

# this = trade route that sells $GOOD$ to a stranger holder whose local pool holds an adult commoner
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_good_sell_holder_pool_route_trigger = {
	is_commission_route = no
	save_temporary_scope_as = tgf_goodsell_route_temp
	any_trade_market_on_route = {
		gdp_tgf_good_sell_holder_market_trigger = {
			ROUTE = scope:tgf_goodsell_route_temp
			GOOD = $GOOD$
		}
		gdp_tgf_good_sell_pool_market_trigger = {
			ROUTE = scope:tgf_goodsell_route_temp
			GOOD = $GOOD$
		}
		gdp_tgf_gruit_market_trigger = yes
	}
}

# Gives root Hop Trader, or its renowned version when renowned in scope:tgf_category, upgrading an existing plain one
scripted_effect gdp_tgf_add_hop_trader_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_hop_trader_renowned_modifier } }
		if = {
			limit = { gdp_tgf_root_renowned_in_event_category_trigger = yes }
			remove_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_hop_trader_modifier
			add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_hop_trader_renowned_modifier
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_hop_trader_modifier } }
			add_character_modifier = gdp_tgf_hop_trader_modifier
		}
	}
}

# Gruit or Hops
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3031 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3031.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3031.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = tavern }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = toast_goblet
		camera = camera_event_left
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_brewer
		animation = drink_goblet
		camera = camera_event_right
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		character = scope:tgf_market_holder
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		any_trade_route = {
			gdp_tgf_good_sell_holder_pool_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_beer }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_trade_route = {
			limit = {
				gdp_tgf_good_sell_holder_pool_route_trigger = { GOOD = trade_good_beer }
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_route
		}
		scope:tgf_route = {
			random_trade_market_on_route = {
				limit = {
					gdp_tgf_good_sell_holder_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						GOOD = trade_good_beer
					}
					gdp_tgf_good_sell_pool_market_trigger = {
						ROUTE = scope:tgf_route
						GOOD = trade_good_beer
					}
					gdp_tgf_gruit_market_trigger = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = tgf_market
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market = {
			trade_market_owner = {
				holder = { save_scope_as = tgf_market_holder }
				title_province = {
					save_scope_as = tgf_source_province
					county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
				}
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:tgf_source_province
			limit = {
				is_healthy = yes
				has_no_particular_noble_roots_trigger = yes
				is_physically_able_adult = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = tgf_brewer
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_beer = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = scope:tgf_brewer
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_market.trade_market_owner.title_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3031.a
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		gdp_tgf_add_hop_trader_effect = yes
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = annoyed_opinion
				opinion = -20
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = content factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3031.b
		scope:tgf_market = {
			add_price_modifier_for_character = {
				type = sell_mult
				character = root
				trade_good_type = scope:tgf_good
				value = { value = 0.01 multiply = gdp_tgf_renown_tier_value }
				years = 10
			}
		}
		scope:tgf_market_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = ambitious factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3031.c
		gdp_increase_trade_expertise_xp_effect = {
			CATEGORY = scope:tgf_category
			VALUE = trade_expertise_xp_major_value
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = stubborn factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}

# this = province where get_region_fruit_plural knows a regional fruit
scripted_trigger gdp_tgf_has_region_fruit_trigger = {
	OR = {
		geographical_region = world_africa
		geographical_region = world_africa_north
		geographical_region = world_africa_sahara
		geographical_region = world_asia_china
		geographical_region = world_asia_japan
		geographical_region = world_asia_korea
		geographical_region = world_asia_minor
		geographical_region = world_asia_sakhalin_hokkaido
		geographical_region = world_asia_southeast
		geographical_region = world_europe
		geographical_region = world_europe_south
		geographical_region = world_himalaya
		geographical_region = world_india
		geographical_region = world_middle_east
		geographical_region = world_steppe
		geographical_region = world_steppe_west
		geographical_region = world_tibet
	}
}

# A Basket of Fruit
gdp_trade_good_flavor.3032 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3032.t
	desc = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3032.desc
	theme = trade
	override_background = { reference = market }

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = eating
		camera = camera_event_left
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_trade_good"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	cooldown = { years = 50 }

	trigger = {
		is_available_allow_travelling = yes
		gdp_tgf_eligible_trigger = yes
		exists = location.county
		location = { gdp_tgf_has_region_fruit_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		location = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_fruit_province
			county = { save_scope_as = tgf_source_county }
		}
		trade_good_type:trade_good_fruit = {
			save_scope_as = tgf_good
			trade_good_need_category = { save_scope_as = tgf_category }
		}
		gdp_tgf_set_widget_good_effect = { GOOD = scope:tgf_good }
		gdp_tgf_set_background_location_effect = {
			CHAR = root
			PROVINCE = scope:tgf_fruit_province
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3032.a
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gdp_tgf_eye_for_fruit_modifier
			years = 20
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = content factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gdp_trade_good_flavor.3032.b
		add_stress = minor_stress_loss
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { has_trait = temperate factor = 0 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		gdp_tgf_clear_widget_good_effect = yes
	}
}
